# LLM Proxy Architecture - Complete Report

## 1. What the LLM Proxy Does

The LLM proxy is a **transparent intermediary** between coding agents (Claude Code, Cursor, custom scripts) and Anthropic's API. It intercepts every LLM request, enforces workspace policies, computes costs, captures traces for the knowledge graph, and optionally injects brain context—all without adding perceptible latency.

**Key capabilities:**
- Transparent SSE passthrough (raw byte forwarding)
- Identity resolution from Claude Code metadata + custom headers
- Graph-native trace capture (llm_call trace nodes with relationship edges)
- Real-time cost computation from per-model pricing tables
- Policy enforcement (model access, daily budget limits, per-workspace rate limiting)
- Brain context injection (decisions, learnings, observations ranked by semantic similarity)
- Conversation tracking with deterministic UUIDv5 hashing
- Complete audit trail via policy decision logging

---

## 2. Database Schema: Messages vs. Traces

### **CONVERSATION** table
```sql
DEFINE TABLE conversation SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD workspace ON conversation TYPE record<workspace>;
DEFINE FIELD messages ON conversation TYPE option<array<record<message>>>;
DEFINE FIELD source ON conversation TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD title ON conversation TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD title_source ON conversation TYPE option<string> ASSERT $value IN ["message", "entity"];
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON conversation TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE FIELD discusses ON conversation TYPE option<record<project | person | feature | task | decision | question | observation | suggestion>>;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON conversation TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD updatedAt ON conversation TYPE datetime;
DEFINE INDEX conversation_workspace ON conversation FIELDS workspace;
```

**Purpose:** Groups related LLM turns into a conversation. In the proxy context, one conversation = all turns with the same (system_prompt + first_user_message) hash.

### **MESSAGE** table
```sql
DEFINE TABLE message SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD conversation ON message TYPE record<conversation>;
DEFINE FIELD role ON message TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["user", "assistant"];
DEFINE FIELD text ON message TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD createdAt ON message TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD suggestions ON message TYPE option<array<string>>;
DEFINE FIELD clientMessageId ON message TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON message TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX message_conversation_createdAt ON message FIELDS conversation, createdAt;
```

**Purpose:** Individual messages within a conversation (user or assistant roles). Currently NOT populated by the proxy—proxy only upserts the `conversation` record itself.

### **TRACE** table (LLM Proxy focus)
```sql
DEFINE TABLE trace SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD type ON trace TYPE string 
  ASSERT $value IN ["tool_call", "message", "subagent_spawn", "intent_submission", "bridge_exchange", "llm_call"];
DEFINE FIELD model ON trace TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD provider ON trace TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD input_tokens ON trace TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD output_tokens ON trace TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD cache_creation_tokens ON trace TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD cache_read_tokens ON trace TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD cost_usd ON trace TYPE option<float>;
DEFINE FIELD latency_ms ON trace TYPE option<int>;
DEFINE FIELD stop_reason ON trace TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD request_id ON trace TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD workspace ON trace TYPE option<record<workspace>>;
DEFINE FIELD session ON trace TYPE option<record<agent_session>>;
DEFINE FIELD actor ON trace TYPE option<record<identity>>;
DEFINE FIELD parent_trace ON trace TYPE option<record<trace>>;
DEFINE FIELD input ON trace TYPE option<object> FLEXIBLE;
DEFINE FIELD output ON trace TYPE option<object> FLEXIBLE;
DEFINE FIELD policy_decision ON trace TYPE option<object> FLEXIBLE;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON trace TYPE datetime;
```

**Purpose:** First-class graph records for every LLM call with full cost/token breakdown.

### **TRACE RELATIONSHIP EDGES**
```sql
-- Scope edge: trace belongs to a workspace
DEFINE TABLE scoped_to TYPE RELATION IN trace OUT workspace;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON scoped_to TYPE datetime;

-- Session invocation: session spawned this trace
DEFINE TABLE invoked TYPE RELATION IN agent_session OUT trace;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON invoked TYPE datetime;

-- Task attribution: trace is attributed to a task
DEFINE TABLE attributed_to TYPE RELATION IN trace OUT task;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON attributed_to TYPE datetime;

-- Policy governance: trace was governed by a policy
DEFINE TABLE governed_by TYPE RELATION IN trace OUT policy;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON governed_by TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD decision ON governed_by TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ['pass', 'deny'];
```

---

## 3. Key Distinction: Traces vs. Messages

| Aspect | Traces (LLM Proxy) | Messages (Chat System) |
|--------|-------------------|----------------------|
| **Purpose** | Cost/audit/governance tracking | Chat history context |
| **Populated by** | LLM proxy after each call | Chat agent when processing user input |
| **Level of detail** | Token counts, cost, latency, policy decision, response content (opaque) | Full conversation text with embeddings |
| **Scope** | Per LLM call (atomic) | Per conversation turn (logical) |
| **Edges** | workspace, session, task, policy | conversation (array reference) |
| **Schema** | SCHEMAFULL, typed fields for tokens/cost | Flexible, embedded in conversation array |
| **Data granularity** | Detailed cost breakdown (input/output/cache) | Just text and metadata |

**The proxy does NOT create messages.** It only:
1. Upserts `conversation` records (deterministic ID from system_prompt + first_user_message)
2. Creates `trace` records with full cost/token breakdown
3. Creates edges linking traces to workspace, session, task, and policy

---

## 4. Data Flow: 9-Step Pipeline

### **Request → Proxy → Response**

```
Agent (Claude Code)
  │
  ├─> POST /proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages
  │     body: { model, stream, system, messages, metadata }
  │     headers: X-Brain-Workspace, X-Brain-Task, X-Brain-Session, X-Api-Key
  │
  ├─> STEP 1: Parse request body
  │     Extract: model, stream flag, system prompt, messages, metadata.user_id
  │
  ├─> STEP 2: Identity Resolution (resolveIdentity)
  │     Input: metadata.user_id + X-Brain-* headers
  │     Output: { workspaceId, taskId, sessionHeader, agentType, userHash, accountId }
  │
  ├─> STEP 3: Session ID Resolution (resolveSessionId + resolveAgentSessionId)
  │     Input: sessionHeader (priority) or external session UUID
  │     Output: effectiveSessionId (agent_session record PK, via DB lookup)
  │
  ├─> STEP 3.5: Conversation Hash (resolveConversationHash) — PURE
  │     Input: system_prompt + messages[0].content (first user message)
  │     Process:
  │       UUIDv5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message)
  │     Output: conversationId (deterministic), title (truncated first message)
  │     Side effect: upsertConversation(conversationId, workspaceId, title)
  │
  ├─> STEP 4: Policy Evaluation (evaluateProxyPolicy)
  │     Checks:
  │       1. Model allowlist (agent_type → allowed models)
  │       2. Daily budget limit (workspace spend < limit)
  │       3. Rate limit (sliding window: 60 req/min per workspace)
  │     Decision:
  │       "allow" → continue
  │       "deny_model" (403) → blocked model
  │       "deny_budget" (429) → daily limit exceeded
  │       "deny_rate_limit" (429) → too many requests
  │
  ├─> STEP 5: Context Injection (runContextInjection) — OPTIONAL
  │     IF intelligenceConfig.contextInjectionEnabled:
  │       1. Load candidate pool from DB:
  │          - 50 recent confirmed decisions
  │          - 30 active learnings
  │          - 20 open observations (severity: conflict/warning)
  │       2. Embed last user message (1536-dim embedding)
  │       3. Rank candidates by weighted cosine similarity:
  │          decision_weight=1.0, learning_weight=0.8, observation_weight=0.7
  │       4. Select top-N within token budget (configurable, e.g., 4000 tokens)
  │       5. Build XML block: <brain-context>...</brain-context>
  │       6. Inject into system prompt (string or array form)
  │       7. Return modified body + injection metadata
  │     IF error or disabled:
  │       Pass through original body (fail-open)
  │
  ├─> STEP 6: Forward to Anthropic (fetch)
  │     Method: POST upstreamUrl (Anthropic API)
  │     Headers: Forwarded from request (anthropic-version, x-api-key, etc.)
  │     Body: effectiveBody (original or context-injected)
  │     Transport: Streaming or non-streaming based on stream flag
  │
  ├─> STEP 7: Response Relay (SSE or JSON)
  │     IF streaming:
  │       TransformStream: raw bytes pass through while extracting SSE tokens
  │       Extract usage from: message_start.usage + message_delta.usage
  │     IF non-streaming:
  │       Buffer response, extract usage from JSON body
  │
  ├─> STEP 8: Async Trace Capture (captureTrace + deps.inflight.track)
  │     Input: TraceData object with:
  │       - model, inputTokens, outputTokens, cacheCreationTokens, cacheReadTokens
  │       - latencyMs, workspaceId, sessionId, taskId, conversationId
  │       - policyDecision (if evaluated)
  │       - intelligenceMetadata (if injected)
  │       - responseContent (opaque, per ADR-051)
  │     Process (async, non-blocking):
  │       1. Compute cost: calculateCost(usage, getModelPricing(model))
  │       2. CREATE trace node with type="llm_call"
  │       3. RELATE edges:
  │          - trace->scoped_to->workspace
  │          - agent_session->invoked->trace
  │          - trace->attributed_to->task
  │          - trace->governed_by->policy
  │
  ├─> RETURN Response to agent (transparent)
  │     Status: upstream.status
  │     Headers: Content-Type, CORS, SSE headers (if streaming)
  │     Body: LLM response (unchanged)
  │
  └─> Agent receives response (indistinguishable from direct Anthropic call)
```

---

## 5. Trace Capture: What Gets Persisted

When a trace is created, the following data is stored:

### **Trace Node Fields** (created in `trace-writer.ts::createTraceNode`)
```typescript
const content = {
  type: "llm_call",
  model: data.model,
  provider: "anthropic",
  input_tokens: data.inputTokens,
  output_tokens: data.outputTokens,
  cache_creation_tokens: data.cacheCreationTokens,
  cache_read_tokens: data.cacheReadTokens,
  cost_usd: costUsd,  // Computed from pricing table
  latency_ms: Math.round(data.latencyMs),
  stop_reason: data.stopReason,
  created_at: new Date(),
  request_id: data.requestId,
  workspace: RecordId("workspace", workspaceId),
  
  // FLEXIBLE input field (stores conversation + intelligence metadata)
  input: {
    conversation: RecordId("conversation", conversationId),
    brain_context_injected: boolean,
    brain_context_decisions: number,
    brain_context_learnings: number,
    brain_context_observations: number,
    brain_context_tokens_est: number,
  },
  
  // FLEXIBLE output field (opaque response capture, per ADR-051)
  output: {
    content_blocks: Array<{ type, text, id, name, input }>,
    stop_reason: string,
    usage: { input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_creation_tokens?, cache_read_tokens? },
  },
  
  // FLEXIBLE policy_decision field
  policy_decision: {
    decision: "pass" | "deny",
    policy_refs: string[],
    reason?: string,
    timestamp: string,
  },
};
```

### **Trace Edges Created**
1. **scoped_to**: `trace->scoped_to->workspace` (always if workspace known)
2. **invoked**: `agent_session->invoked->trace` (if session resolved)
3. **attributed_to**: `trace->attributed_to->task` (if task ID provided)
4. **governed_by**: `trace->governed_by->policy` (if policy evaluated, one edge per policy ID)

---

## 6. Message Capture: What the Proxy Does NOT Do

The proxy **does NOT** populate the `message` table. That's the chat agent's job:

1. **Chat agent** (`chat/chat-processor.ts`) receives user input → creates `message` records
2. **LLM proxy** captures `trace` records (for cost/governance/audit)
3. **Conversation** table links them: `conversation.messages = [message1, message2, ...]`

The proxy only upserts the `conversation` record itself, with:
- `conversationId` = UUIDv5(system_prompt + first_user_message)
- `title` = truncated first user message
- `workspace` = resolved workspace
- `source` = "proxy"

---

## 7. Conversation Hashing: Deterministic Stability

**Purpose**: Group all turns in the same conversation (same system prompt + initial question) under one stable ID, enabling trace grouping across multiple turns without session lifecycle management.

**Algorithm** (in `conversation-hash-resolver.ts`):
```typescript
const BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE = "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d";
const hashContent = systemPrompt + NUL_SEPARATOR + firstUserMessage;
const conversationId = generateUuidV5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, hashContent);
```

**Properties:**
- Deterministic: same system prompt + first message = same ID every time
- Stable: works across multiple turns without requiring session tracking
- No external state: purely content-based, no DB lookups needed
- Title derived from first user message (truncated to ~100 chars)

---

## 8. Context Injection: How Brain Knowledge Flows into Requests

**Flow** (in `anthropic-proxy-route.ts::runContextInjection`):

1. **Load candidate pool** (DB queries, cached per workspace for 5 min):
   - `SELECT ... FROM decision WHERE workspace=$ws AND status='confirmed' LIMIT 50`
   - `SELECT ... FROM learning WHERE workspace=$ws AND status='active' LIMIT 30`
   - `SELECT ... FROM observation WHERE workspace=$ws AND status='open' AND severity IN ['conflict','warning'] LIMIT 20`

2. **Embed last user message** (AI SDK embedding):
   - Take last `{ role: "user", content }` message from request
   - Compute 1536-dim embedding via configured model

3. **Rank by weighted similarity** (pure function):
   ```typescript
   score = cosineSimilarity(candidate.embedding, queryEmbedding) * weight
   // weights: decision=1.0, learning=0.8, observation=0.7
   ```

4. **Select within token budget**:
   - Sort by score descending
   - Accumulate tokens until budget exhausted (e.g., 4000 tokens)

5. **Build XML block** (pure function):
   ```xml
   <brain-context>
     <decision id="..." similarity="...">
       {decision.summary}
     </decision>
     <learning id="..." similarity="...">
       {learning.text}
     </learning>
     ...
   </brain-context>
   ```

6. **Inject into system prompt**:
   - If system is string: prepend XML
   - If system is array: insert as new text block or merge with first block (cache control aware)

7. **Return modified body**: `{ ...request, system: injectedSystem }`

**Error handling**: If anything fails (missing embedding, empty pool, embedding error), fall back to original body (fail-open).

---

## 9. Cost Calculation: Pricing Model

**Algorithm** (in `cost-calculator.ts`):

```typescript
uncachedInput = inputTokens - cacheReadTokens - cacheCreationTokens
cost = (uncachedInput * input_rate 
        + cacheReadTokens * cache_read_rate
        + cacheCreationTokens * cache_creation_rate
        + outputTokens * output_rate) / 1_000_000
```

**Pricing table** (static, in `pricing-table.ts`):
```typescript
getModelPricing("claude-sonnet-4-20250514") → {
  inputPerMillion: 3_000,           // $3 per 1M input tokens
  outputPerMillion: 15_000,         // $15 per 1M output tokens
  cacheReadPerMillion: 375,         // 12.5% of input rate
  cacheCreationPerMillion: 3_750,   // 125% of input rate
}
```

**Stored in trace**: `cost_usd` (exact float value for audit trail)

## 11. Summary Table

| Aspect | Detail |
|--------|--------|
| **Entry point** | `POST /proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages` |
| **Pipeline steps** | 9 (identity → session → hash → policy → injection → forward → relay → trace → response) |
| **Primary tables** | trace (llm_call nodes), conversation (grouped turns), agent_session (session tracking) |
| **Not populated** | message table (that's the chat agent's job) |
| **Conversation ID** | UUIDv5(namespace, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message) — deterministic, stable |
| **Trace persistence** | Async, non-blocking, tracked via inflight.track() to avoid blocking response delivery |
| **Cost breakdown** | Stored in trace.cost_usd, computed from token counts × model pricing table |
| **Context injection** | Optional, KNN search for decisions/learnings/observations ranked by cosine similarity |
| **Policy enforcement** | Model allowlist, daily budget limit, per-workspace rate limit (60 req/min) |
| **Error handling** | Fail-open (continue without injection if any step fails); fallback logging on trace write failure |
| **Identity resolution** | From Claude Code metadata.user_id + X-Brain-* headers (workspace, task, session, agent type) |

> AGENT

Good question. Here's where things stand and the trade-offs:

## Current State

| What | Stored? | Where |
|------|---------|-------|
| Token counts, cost, latency | Yes | `trace` (type=`llm_call`) |
| Response content blocks | Yes | `trace.output` (FLEXIBLE) |
| Conversation grouping | Yes | `conversation` (UUIDv5 hash) |
| Individual user/assistant messages | **No** | `message` table unused by proxy |

The proxy upserts a `conversation` record and creates a `trace` per LLM call, but never writes to `message`.

## Key Trade-offs

**For storing messages:**
- Full conversation replay (reconstruct what the agent said/asked)
- Extraction pipeline could run on proxy messages — surface decisions, observations from coding agent conversations
- Searchable across all agent conversations, not just chat UI
- Complete the `conversation → message[]` link that already exists in schema

**Against:**
- **Volume** — coding agents send massive context windows (100k+ tokens per turn with tool results, codebase content)
- The `message` schema has `text: string` — designed for chat UI, not multi-block Anthropic messages with tool_use/tool_result arrays
- `trace.output` already captures response content blocks
- Privacy — agent conversations may contain credentials, .env contents, etc.

## The real question

Is this about **audit/replay** (you want to see exactly what was sent/received) or **knowledge extraction** (you want the extraction pipeline to mine coding agent conversations for decisions/entities)?

Those lead to very different designs:
- **Audit**: store raw request/response in trace (already partially done via `trace.output`)
- **Extraction**: store just the text content in `message` and wire up the extraction pipeline

What's the motivation here?

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Conversation Upserter — Idempotent DB Adapter
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates or updates a conversation record in SurrealDB using native UPSERT
     5→ * with the deterministic UUIDv5 conversation ID from the hash resolver.
     6→ *
     7→ * Port: (ConversationUpsertInput, Dependencies) -> Promise<RecordId | undefined>
     8→ * Side effects: SurrealDB writes (boundary adapter)
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    12→import type { Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    13→import { logInfo, logError } from "../http/observability";
    14→
    15→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→// Types
    17→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→
    19→export type ConversationUpsertInput = {
    20→  readonly conversationId: string;
    21→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    22→  readonly title: string;
    23→};
    24→
    25→type ConversationUpsertDependencies = {
    26→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    27→};
    28→
    29→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→// Public API
    31→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Upsert a conversation record. Returns the RecordId on success,
    35→ * undefined on failure. Failure never throws -- it logs a warning
    36→ * and returns undefined so the caller can proceed without blocking.
    37→ */
    38→export async function upsertConversation(
    39→  input: ConversationUpsertInput,
    40→  deps: ConversationUpsertDependencies,
    41→): Promise<RecordId | undefined> {
    42→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", input.conversationId);
    43→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", input.workspaceId);
    44→
    45→  try {
    46→    const results = await deps.surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>(
    47→      `UPSERT $conversation MERGE {
    48→        workspace: $workspace,
    49→        title: $title,
    50→        title_source: "message",
    51→        source: "proxy",
    52→        createdAt: createdAt ?? time::now(),
    53→        updatedAt: time::now()
    54→      };`,
    55→      {
    56→        conversation: conversationRecord,
    57→        workspace: workspaceRecord,
    58→        title: input.title,
    59→      },
    60→    );
    61→
    62→    const record = results[0]?.[0];
    63→    if (record) {
    64→      logInfo("proxy.conversation.upserted", "Proxy conversation upserted", {
    65→        conversation_id: input.conversationId,
    66→        workspace_id: input.workspaceId,
    67→      });
    68→      return record.id;
    69→    }
    70→  } catch (error) {
    71→    logError(
    72→      "proxy.conversation.upsert_failed",
    73→      "Failed to upsert conversation record",
    74→      error,
    75→    );
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  return undefined;
    79→}
    80→

     1→/**
     2→ * Conversation Hash Resolver — Pure Function
     3→ *
     4→ * Derives a deterministic conversation identity from request content
     5→ * using UUIDv5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message).
     6→ *
     7→ * This enables trace grouping across multiple turns of the same conversation
     8→ * without requiring Brain integration or session lifecycle management.
     9→ *
    10→ * Port: ConversationHashInput -> ConversationHashResult | undefined
    11→ * Side effects: none (pure function)
    12→ */
    13→
    14→import { createHash } from "crypto";
    15→
    16→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→// UUIDv5 Namespace for Brain Proxy
    18→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→/** Brain proxy namespace UUID for UUIDv5 generation (randomly generated, fixed). */
    21→const BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE = "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d";
    22→
    23→/** NUL separator between system prompt and first user message. */
    24→const NUL_SEPARATOR = "\0";
    25→
    26→/** Maximum title length before truncation. */
    27→const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 100;
    28→
    29→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→// Types
    31→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→export type ConversationHashInput = {
    34→  readonly systemPrompt?: string;
    35→  readonly systemPromptBlocks?: ReadonlyArray<{ type: string; text: string }>;
    36→  readonly messages: ReadonlyArray<{ role: string; content: string }>;
    37→};
    38→
    39→export type ConversationHashResult = {
    40→  readonly conversationId: string;
    41→  readonly title: string;
    42→};
    43→
    44→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→// UUIDv5 Implementation (RFC 4122)
    46→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→
    48→function namespaceToBytes(namespaceUuid: string): Buffer {
    49→  const hex = namespaceUuid.replace(/-/g, "");
    50→  return Buffer.from(hex, "hex");
    51→}
    52→
    53→function generateUuidV5(namespace: string, name: string): string {
    54→  const namespaceBytes = namespaceToBytes(namespace);
    55→  const nameBytes = Buffer.from(name, "utf-8");
    56→
    57→  const hashInput = Buffer.concat([namespaceBytes, nameBytes]);
    58→  const hash = createHash("sha1").update(hashInput).digest();
    59→
    60→  // Set version to 5 (bits 4-7 of byte 6)
    61→  hash[6] = (hash[6] & 0x0f) | 0x50;
    62→
    63→  // Set variant to RFC 4122 (bits 6-7 of byte 8)
    64→  hash[8] = (hash[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
    65→
    66→  // Format as UUID string (use first 16 bytes of SHA-1)
    67→  const hex = hash.subarray(0, 16).toString("hex");
    68→  return [
    69→    hex.slice(0, 8),
    70→    hex.slice(8, 12),
    71→    hex.slice(12, 16),
    72→    hex.slice(16, 20),
    73→    hex.slice(20, 32),
    74→  ].join("-");
    75→}
    76→
    77→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→// Title Truncation
    79→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→
    81→export function truncateTitle(text: string, maxLength: number = MAX_TITLE_LENGTH): string {
    82→  if (text.length <= maxLength) return text;
    83→  return text.slice(0, maxLength) + "...";
    84→}
    85→
    86→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    87→// System Prompt Normalization
    88→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→
    90→function normalizeSystemPrompt(input: ConversationHashInput): string | undefined {
    91→  if (input.systemPrompt) return input.systemPrompt;
    92→
    93→  if (input.systemPromptBlocks && input.systemPromptBlocks.length > 0) {
    94→    const textBlocks = input.systemPromptBlocks
    95→      .filter((block) => block.type === "text")
    96→      .map((block) => block.text);
    97→    return textBlocks.length > 0 ? textBlocks.join("\n") : undefined;
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  return undefined;
   101→}
   102→
   103→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→// First User Message Extraction
   105→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→function extractFirstUserMessage(
   108→  messages: ReadonlyArray<{ role: string; content: string }>,
   109→): string | undefined {
   110→  const firstUserMsg = messages.find((msg) => msg.role === "user");
   111→  return firstUserMsg?.content;
   112→}
   113→
   114→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→// Public API
   116→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→/**
   119→ * Resolve a deterministic conversation identity from request content.
   120→ *
   121→ * Returns undefined when either the system prompt or the first user message
   122→ * is missing -- these are required to compute a stable hash.
   123→ *
   124→ * The conversation ID is a UUIDv5 derived from:
   125→ *   UUIDv5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message)
   126→ *
   127→ * The title is the first user message, truncated to ~100 characters.
   128→ */
   129→export function resolveConversationHash(
   130→  input: ConversationHashInput,
   131→): ConversationHashResult | undefined {
   132→  const systemPrompt = normalizeSystemPrompt(input);
   133→  if (!systemPrompt) return undefined;
   134→
   135→  const firstUserMessage = extractFirstUserMessage(input.messages);
   136→  if (!firstUserMessage) return undefined;
   137→
   138→  const hashContent = systemPrompt + NUL_SEPARATOR + firstUserMessage;
   139→  const conversationId = generateUuidV5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, hashContent);
   140→  const title = truncateTitle(firstUserMessage);
   141→
   142→  return { conversationId, title };
   143→}
   144→

     1→/**
     2→ * Trace Writer — Async trace capture for LLM proxy calls
     3→ *
     4→ * Creates llm_call trace nodes in SurrealDB after stream/response completion.
     5→ * All graph writes are async via deps.inflight.track() to avoid blocking
     6→ * response delivery.
     7→ *
     8→ * Port: (TraceData, Dependencies) -> Promise<void>
     9→ * Side effects: SurrealDB writes (boundary adapter)
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    13→import { logInfo, logError } from "../http/observability";
    14→import { calculateCost, type TokenUsage } from "./cost-calculator";
    15→import { getModelPricing } from "./pricing-table";
    16→import { withRetry } from "./retry";
    17→import type { Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    18→
    19→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→// Types
    21→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→export type TraceData = {
    24→  readonly model: string;
    25→  readonly inputTokens: number;
    26→  readonly outputTokens: number;
    27→  readonly cacheCreationTokens: number;
    28→  readonly cacheReadTokens: number;
    29→  readonly stopReason?: string;
    30→  readonly latencyMs: number;
    31→  readonly workspaceId?: string;
    32→  readonly sessionId?: string;
    33→  readonly taskId?: string;
    34→  readonly requestId?: string;
    35→  readonly conversationId?: string;
    36→  readonly policyDecision?: {
    37→    readonly decision: "pass" | "deny";
    38→    readonly policy_refs: string[];
    39→    readonly reason?: string;
    40→    readonly timestamp: string;
    41→  };
    42→  // Intelligence metadata (context injection)
    43→  readonly intelligenceMetadata?: {
    44→    readonly brain_context_injected: boolean;
    45→    readonly brain_context_decisions: number;
    46→    readonly brain_context_learnings: number;
    47→    readonly brain_context_observations: number;
    48→    readonly brain_context_tokens_est: number;
    49→  };
    50→  // Response content (opaque capture per ADR-051)
    51→  readonly responseContent?: {
    52→    readonly content_blocks: Array<{ type: string; text?: string; id?: string; name?: string; input?: unknown }>;
    53→    readonly stop_reason: string;
    54→    readonly usage: {
    55→      readonly input_tokens: number;
    56→      readonly output_tokens: number;
    57→      readonly cache_creation_tokens?: number;
    58→      readonly cache_read_tokens?: number;
    59→    };
    60→  };
    61→};
    62→
    63→type TraceDependencies = {
    64→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    65→};
    66→
    67→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→// Trace Node Creation
    69→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    70→
    71→async function createTraceNode(
    72→  surreal: Surreal,
    73→  traceId: string,
    74→  data: TraceData,
    75→  costUsd: number,
    76→): Promise<RecordId> {
    77→  const traceRecord = new RecordId("trace", traceId);
    78→
    79→  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {
    80→    type: "llm_call",
    81→    model: data.model,
    82→    provider: "anthropic",
    83→    input_tokens: data.inputTokens,
    84→    output_tokens: data.outputTokens,
    85→    cache_creation_tokens: data.cacheCreationTokens,
    86→    cache_read_tokens: data.cacheReadTokens,
    87→    cost_usd: costUsd,
    88→    latency_ms: Math.round(data.latencyMs),
    89→    stop_reason: data.stopReason ?? "end_turn",
    90→    created_at: new Date(),
    91→  };
    92→
    93→  // Set workspace directly on the trace node when known
    94→  if (data.workspaceId) {
    95→    content.workspace = new RecordId("workspace", data.workspaceId);
    96→  }
    97→
    98→  if (data.requestId) {
    99→    content.request_id = data.requestId;
   100→  }
   101→
   102→  // Store conversation reference and intelligence metadata in FLEXIBLE input field
   103→  {
   104→    const inputData: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   105→
   106→    if (data.conversationId) {
   107→      inputData.conversation = new RecordId("conversation", data.conversationId);
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    if (data.intelligenceMetadata) {
   111→      inputData.brain_context_injected = data.intelligenceMetadata.brain_context_injected;
   112→      inputData.brain_context_decisions = data.intelligenceMetadata.brain_context_decisions;
   113→      inputData.brain_context_learnings = data.intelligenceMetadata.brain_context_learnings;
   114→      inputData.brain_context_observations = data.intelligenceMetadata.brain_context_observations;
   115→      inputData.brain_context_tokens_est = data.intelligenceMetadata.brain_context_tokens_est;
   116→    }
   117→
   118→    if (Object.keys(inputData).length > 0) {
   119→      content.input = inputData;
   120→    }
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  // Store response content in FLEXIBLE output field (opaque capture per ADR-051)
   124→  if (data.responseContent) {
   125→    content.output = data.responseContent;
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  if (data.policyDecision) {
   129→    content.policy_decision = data.policyDecision;
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $trace CONTENT $content;`, {
   133→    trace: traceRecord,
   134→    content,
   135→  });
   136→
   137→  return traceRecord;
   138→}
   139→
   140→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→// Edge Creation
   142→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   143→
   144→async function createTraceEdges(
   145→  surreal: Surreal,
   146→  traceRecord: RecordId,
   147→  data: TraceData,
   148→): Promise<void> {
   149→  // Always create workspace scope edge when workspace is known
   150→  if (data.workspaceId) {
   151→    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", data.workspaceId);
   152→    await surreal.query(
   153→      `RELATE $trace->scoped_to->$workspace SET created_at = time::now();`,
   154→      { trace: traceRecord, workspace: workspaceRecord },
   155→    );
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  // Create session invocation edge when session is resolved.
   159→  // NOTE: `$session` is a SurrealDB protected variable — use `$sess` instead.
   160→  // The caller must resolve the effective session ID to an agent_session PK
   161→  // before passing it here.
   162→  if (data.sessionId) {
   163→    const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", data.sessionId);
   164→    await surreal.query(
   165→      `RELATE $sess->invoked->$trace SET created_at = time::now();`,
   166→      { sess: sessionRecord, trace: traceRecord },
   167→    );
   168→  }
   169→
   170→  // Create task attribution edge when task is resolved
   171→  if (data.taskId) {
   172→    const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", data.taskId);
   173→    await surreal.query(
   174→      `RELATE $trace->attributed_to->$task SET created_at = time::now();`,
   175→      { trace: traceRecord, task: taskRecord },
   176→    );
   177→  }
   178→
   179→  // Create governed_by edges for policy audit trail
   180→  if (data.policyDecision) {
   181→    for (const policyId of data.policyDecision.policy_refs) {
   182→      const policyRecord = new RecordId("policy", policyId);
   183→      await surreal.query(
   184→        `RELATE $trace->governed_by->$policy SET created_at = time::now(), decision = $decision;`,
   185→        {
   186→          trace: traceRecord,
   187→          policy: policyRecord,
   188→          decision: data.policyDecision.decision,
   189→        },
   190→      );
   191→    }
   192→  }
   193→}
   194→
   195→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→// Public API
   197→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198→
   199→/**
   200→ * Capture an LLM call trace asynchronously.
   201→ *
   202→ * Computes cost from the pricing table, creates a trace node, and
   203→ * establishes relationship edges. All operations are retried 3x with
   204→ * exponential backoff. On persistent failure, logs structured output
   205→ * instead of throwing.
   206→ *
   207→ * This function returns a Promise that should be tracked via
   208→ * deps.inflight.track() — it must NOT block response delivery.
   209→ */
   210→export async function captureTrace(
   211→  data: TraceData,
   212→  deps: TraceDependencies,
   213→): Promise<void> {
   214→  const traceId = crypto.randomUUID();
   215→
   216→  // Compute cost from pricing table (pure)
   217→  const usage: TokenUsage = {
   218→    inputTokens: data.inputTokens,
   219→    outputTokens: data.outputTokens,
   220→    cacheCreationTokens: data.cacheCreationTokens,
   221→    cacheReadTokens: data.cacheReadTokens,
   222→  };
   223→  const pricing = getModelPricing(data.model);
   224→  const costUsd = calculateCost(usage, pricing);
   225→
   226→  try {
   227→    // Create trace node with retry
   228→    const traceRecord = await withRetry(
   229→      () => createTraceNode(deps.surreal, traceId, data, costUsd),
   230→      "trace_node_create",
   231→    );
   232→
   233→    // Create edges with retry
   234→    await withRetry(
   235→      () => createTraceEdges(deps.surreal, traceRecord, data),
   236→      "trace_edges_create",
   237→    );
   238→
   239→    logInfo("proxy.trace.captured", "LLM call trace captured", {
   240→      trace_id: traceId,
   241→      model: data.model,
   242→      cost_usd: costUsd,
   243→      workspace_id: data.workspaceId,
   244→    });
   245→  } catch (error) {
   246→    // Fallback: structured log output when graph write fails after retries
   247→    logError("proxy.trace.write_failed", "Failed to write trace after retries, logging fallback", error);
   248→    logInfo("proxy.trace.fallback", "Trace data (graph write failed)", {
   249→      trace_id: traceId,
   250→      model: data.model,
   251→      input_tokens: data.inputTokens,
   252→      output_tokens: data.outputTokens,
   253→      cache_creation_tokens: data.cacheCreationTokens,
   254→      cache_read_tokens: data.cacheReadTokens,
   255→      cost_usd: costUsd,
   256→      latency_ms: Math.round(data.latencyMs),
   257→      stop_reason: data.stopReason,
   258→      workspace_id: data.workspaceId,
   259→      session_id: data.sessionId,
   260→      task_id: data.taskId,
   261→    });
   262→  }
   263→}
   264→
```

> AGENT

Yes, exactly. The trace already links to `agent_session` via the `invoked` edge — that's the correct grouping for proxy calls. The `conversation` upsert is polluting a chat UI table with proxy data.

Let me see how the main route orchestrates this so I can map out the removal.

     1→/**
     2→ * Anthropic LLM Proxy Route
     3→ *
     4→ * Transparent proxy that forwards requests to Anthropic's Messages API
     5→ * with identity resolution, session tracking, and workspace validation.
     6→ *
     7→ * Pipeline execution order:
     8→ * 1. Identity resolution (from metadata + headers)
     9→ * 2. Session ID resolution (header priority over metadata)
    10→ * 3. Conversation hash (03-01) — deterministic UUIDv5 from content
    11→ * 4. Policy evaluation [future 02-01]
    12→ * 5. Context injection [future 03-02]
    13→ * 6. Request forwarding
    14→ * 7. Async trace capture (01-03) + conversation upsert
    15→ */
    16→import { logInfo, logError, logWarn, elapsedMs } from "../http/observability";
    17→import { jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
    18→import { resolveIdentity } from "./identity-resolver";
    19→import { resolveSessionId, resolveAgentSessionId } from "./session-id-resolver";
    20→import { captureTrace, type TraceData } from "./trace-writer";
    21→import {
    22→  resolveConversationHash,
    23→  type ConversationHashInput,
    24→} from "./conversation-hash-resolver";
    25→import { upsertConversation } from "./conversation-upserter";
    26→import {
    27→  evaluateProxyPolicy,
    28→  type ProxyPolicyDependencies,
    29→  type ProxyPolicyResult,
    30→  type PolicyDecisionLog,
    31→  type SpendCache,
    32→} from "./policy-evaluator";
    33→import {
    34→  createRateLimiterState,
    35→  pruneStaleEntries,
    36→  type RateLimiterState,
    37→} from "./rate-limiter";
    38→import {
    39→  loadIntelligenceConfig,
    40→  type IntelligenceConfig,
    41→} from "./intelligence-config";
    42→import {
    43→  createContextCache,
    44→  type ContextCache,
    45→  type CachedCandidatePool,
    46→  type CandidateItem,
    47→} from "./context-cache";
    48→import {
    49→  rankCandidates,
    50→  selectWithinBudget,
    51→  buildBrainContextXml,
    52→  injectBrainContext,
    53→  type ContextCandidate,
    54→  type InjectionResult,
    55→} from "./context-injector";
    56→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    57→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    58→
    59→const FORWARDED_HEADERS = [
    60→  "anthropic-version",
    61→  "anthropic-beta",
    62→  "content-type",
    63→] as const;
    64→
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→// Workspace Validation (cached)
    67→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    68→
    69→type WorkspaceCache = Map<string, { valid: boolean; checkedAt: number }>;
    70→
    71→const WORKSPACE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60_000; // 1 minute
    72→
    73→async function validateWorkspace(
    74→  surreal: ServerDependencies["surreal"],
    75→  workspaceId: string,
    76→  cache: WorkspaceCache,
    77→): Promise<boolean> {
    78→  const cached = cache.get(workspaceId);
    79→  if (cached && Date.now() - cached.checkedAt < WORKSPACE_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    80→    return cached.valid;
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  try {
    84→    const results = await surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>(
    85→      `SELECT id FROM $ws;`,
    86→      { ws: new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId) },
    87→    );
    88→    const valid = (results[0]?.length ?? 0) > 0;
    89→    cache.set(workspaceId, { valid, checkedAt: Date.now() });
    90→    return valid;
    91→  } catch {
    92→    cache.set(workspaceId, { valid: false, checkedAt: Date.now() });
    93→    return false;
    94→  }
    95→}
    96→
    97→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→// Header Forwarding
    99→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→function buildUpstreamHeaders(request: Request): Headers {
   102→  const headers = new Headers();
   103→
   104→  for (const name of FORWARDED_HEADERS) {
   105→    const value = request.headers.get(name);
   106→    if (value) headers.set(name, value);
   107→  }
   108→
   109→  const xApiKey = request.headers.get("x-api-key");
   110→  const authHeader = request.headers.get("authorization");
   111→  if (xApiKey) headers.set("x-api-key", xApiKey);
   112→  if (authHeader) headers.set("authorization", authHeader);
   113→
   114→  return headers;
   115→}
   116→
   117→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→// Request Body Parsing
   119→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→
   121→type ParsedBody = {
   122→  model?: string;
   123→  stream?: boolean;
   124→  max_tokens?: number;
   125→  metadata?: { user_id?: string };
   126→  system?: string | Array<{ type: string; text: string }>;
   127→  messages?: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>;
   128→};
   129→
   130→function tryParseRequestBody(body: string): ParsedBody | undefined {
   131→  try {
   132→    return JSON.parse(body) as ParsedBody;
   133→  } catch {
   134→    return undefined;
   135→  }
   136→}
   137→
   138→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139→// Path Detection
   140→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→
   142→function isCountTokensRequest(pathname: string): boolean {
   143→  return pathname.endsWith("/count_tokens");
   144→}
   145→
   146→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→// SSE Usage Extraction
   148→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→
   150→type StreamContext = {
   151→  model?: string;
   152→  inputTokens: number;
   153→  outputTokens: number;
   154→  cacheCreationTokens: number;
   155→  cacheReadTokens: number;
   156→  stopReason?: string;
   157→};
   158→
   159→function extractSSEUsage(buffer: string, ctx: StreamContext): string {
   160→  const lines = buffer.split("\n");
   161→  const remainder = lines.pop() ?? "";
   162→
   163→  for (const line of lines) {
   164→    if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
   165→    const data = line.slice(6);
   166→    if (data === "[DONE]") continue;
   167→
   168→    try {
   169→      const event = JSON.parse(data);
   170→
   171→      if (event.type === "message_start" && event.message?.usage) {
   172→        ctx.inputTokens = event.message.usage.input_tokens ?? 0;
   173→        ctx.cacheCreationTokens = event.message.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens ?? 0;
   174→        ctx.cacheReadTokens = event.message.usage.cache_read_input_tokens ?? 0;
   175→        ctx.model = event.message.model ?? ctx.model;
   176→      }
   177→
   178→      if (event.type === "message_delta") {
   179→        if (event.usage?.output_tokens) {
   180→          ctx.outputTokens = event.usage.output_tokens;
   181→        }
   182→        if (event.delta?.stop_reason) {
   183→          ctx.stopReason = event.delta.stop_reason;
   184→        }
   185→      }
   186→    } catch {
   187→      // partial JSON or non-JSON data line — skip
   188→    }
   189→  }
   190→
   191→  return remainder;
   192→}
   193→
   194→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   195→// Non-Streaming Usage Extraction
   196→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→
   198→type NonStreamingResponse = {
   199→  model?: string;
   200→  usage?: {
   201→    input_tokens?: number;
   202→    output_tokens?: number;
   203→    cache_creation_input_tokens?: number;
   204→    cache_read_input_tokens?: number;
   205→  };
   206→  stop_reason?: string;
   207→};
   208→
   209→function extractNonStreamingUsage(
   210→  responseBody: string,
   211→  requestModel: string | undefined,
   212→  latencyMs: number,
   213→  identity: { workspaceId?: string; taskId?: string },
   214→  sessionId?: string,
   215→  policyDecision?: PolicyDecisionLog,
   216→  conversationId?: string,
   217→  injectionResult?: InjectionResult,
   218→): TraceData | undefined {
   219→  try {
   220→    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody) as NonStreamingResponse;
   221→    if (!parsed.usage) return undefined;
   222→
   223→    const traceData: TraceData = {
   224→      model: parsed.model ?? requestModel ?? "unknown",
   225→      inputTokens: parsed.usage.input_tokens ?? 0,
   226→      outputTokens: parsed.usage.output_tokens ?? 0,
   227→      cacheCreationTokens: parsed.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens ?? 0,
   228→      cacheReadTokens: parsed.usage.cache_read_input_tokens ?? 0,
   229→      stopReason: parsed.stop_reason,
   230→      latencyMs,
   231→      workspaceId: identity.workspaceId,
   232→      sessionId,
   233→      taskId: identity.taskId,
   234→      policyDecision,
   235→      conversationId,
   236→    };
   237→
   238→    // Add intelligence metadata if injection occurred
   239→    if (injectionResult) {
   240→      (traceData as any).intelligenceMetadata = buildIntelligenceMetadata(injectionResult);
   241→    }
   242→
   243→    // Capture response content (opaque, per ADR-051)
   244→    const responseContent = extractResponseContent(responseBody);
   245→    if (responseContent) {
   246→      (traceData as any).responseContent = responseContent;
   247→    }
   248→
   249→    return traceData;
   250→  } catch {
   251→    return undefined;
   252→  }
   253→}
   254→
   255→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→// Context Candidate Pool Loader (adapter boundary)
   257→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   258→
   259→const DECISION_WEIGHT = 1.0;
   260→const LEARNING_WEIGHT = 0.8;
   261→const OBSERVATION_WEIGHT = 0.7;
   262→
   263→async function loadCandidatePool(
   264→  surreal: ServerDependencies["surreal"],
   265→  workspaceId: string,
   266→): Promise<CachedCandidatePool> {
   267→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   268→
   269→  type DecisionRow = { id: RecordId; summary: string; embedding?: number[] };
   270→  type LearningRow = { id: RecordId; text: string; embedding?: number[] };
   271→  type ObservationRow = { id: RecordId; text: string; embedding?: number[] };
   272→
   273→  // Sequential queries to avoid SurrealDB SDK concurrency issues with
   274→  // multiple parallel queries on a single WebSocket connection
   275→  const decisionResults = await surreal.query<[DecisionRow[]]>(
   276→    `SELECT id, summary, embedding FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND status = 'confirmed' LIMIT 50;`,
   277→    { ws: workspaceRecord },
   278→  );
   279→  const learningResults = await surreal.query<[LearningRow[]]>(
   280→    `SELECT id, text, embedding FROM learning WHERE workspace = $ws AND status = 'active' LIMIT 30;`,
   281→    { ws: workspaceRecord },
   282→  );
   283→  const observationResults = await surreal.query<[ObservationRow[]]>(
   284→    `SELECT id, text, embedding FROM observation WHERE workspace = $ws AND status = 'open' AND severity IN ['conflict', 'warning'] AND observation_type NOT IN ['proxy_no_policy'] LIMIT 20;`,
   285→    { ws: workspaceRecord },
   286→  );
   287→
   288→  function toCandidates<T extends { id: RecordId; embedding?: number[] }>(
   289→    rows: T[],
   290→    type: CandidateItem["type"],
   291→    weight: number,
   292→    textFn: (row: T) => string,
   293→  ): CandidateItem[] {
   294→    return rows.map((row) => ({
   295→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   296→      type,
   297→      text: textFn(row),
   298→      weight,
   299→      embedding: row.embedding,
   300→    }));
   301→  }
   302→
   303→  const decisions = toCandidates(decisionResults[0] ?? [], "decision", DECISION_WEIGHT, (d) => d.summary);
   304→  const learnings = toCandidates(learningResults[0] ?? [], "learning", LEARNING_WEIGHT, (l) => l.text);
   305→  const observations = toCandidates(observationResults[0] ?? [], "observation", OBSERVATION_WEIGHT, (o) => o.text);
   306→
   307→  return {
   308→    decisions,
   309→    learnings,
   310→    observations,
   311→    populatedAt: Date.now(),
   312→  };
   313→}
   314→
   315→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   316→// Embedding Helper (adapter boundary)
   317→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→
   319→async function embedUserMessage(
   320→  embeddingModel: ServerDependencies["embeddingModel"],
   321→  embeddingDimension: number,
   322→  text: string,
   323→): Promise<number[] | undefined> {
   324→  try {
   325→    const { embed } = await import("ai");
   326→    const normalized = text.trim();
   327→    if (normalized.length === 0) return undefined;
   328→
   329→    const result = await embed({
   330→      model: embeddingModel,
   331→      value: normalized,
   332→    });
   333→
   334→    if (result.embedding.length !== embeddingDimension) return undefined;
   335→    return result.embedding;
   336→  } catch {
   337→    return undefined;
   338→  }
   339→}
   340→
   341→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   342→// Context Injection Pipeline (orchestrator)
   343→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   344→
   345→type ContextInjectionResult = {
   346→  readonly body: string;
   347→  readonly injectionResult?: InjectionResult;
   348→};
   349→
   350→async function runContextInjection(
   351→  deps: ServerDependencies,
   352→  workspaceId: string,
   353→  parsedBody: ParsedBody,
   354→  originalBody: string,
   355→  contextCache: ContextCache,
   356→  intelligenceConfig: IntelligenceConfig,
   357→): Promise<ContextInjectionResult> {
   358→  if (!intelligenceConfig.contextInjectionEnabled) {
   359→    return { body: originalBody };
   360→  }
   361→
   362→  // 1. Get or populate candidate pool (with cache)
   363→  let pool: CachedCandidatePool;
   364→  let fromCache = false;
   365→  if (contextCache.has(workspaceId)) {
   366→    pool = contextCache.get(workspaceId)!;
   367→    fromCache = true;
   368→  } else {
   369→    pool = await loadCandidatePool(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
   370→    contextCache.set(workspaceId, pool);
   371→  }
   372→
   373→  // 2. Check if pool is empty
   374→  const allCandidates: ContextCandidate[] = [
   375→    ...pool.decisions,
   376→    ...pool.learnings,
   377→    ...pool.observations,
   378→  ];
   379→  if (allCandidates.length === 0) {
   380→    return { body: originalBody };
   381→  }
   382→
   383→  logInfo("proxy.context_injection.pool_loaded", "Candidate pool loaded", {
   384→    workspace_id: workspaceId,
   385→    decisions: pool.decisions.length,
   386→    learnings: pool.learnings.length,
   387→    observations: pool.observations.length,
   388→    total: allCandidates.length,
   389→    cached: fromCache,
   390→  });
   391→
   392→  // 3. Extract last user message for embedding
   393→  const lastUserMessage = [...(parsedBody.messages ?? [])].reverse().find((m) => m.role === "user");
   394→  if (!lastUserMessage) {
   395→    return { body: originalBody };
   396→  }
   397→
   398→  // 4. Embed last user message
   399→  const queryEmbedding = await embedUserMessage(
   400→    deps.embeddingModel,
   401→    deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   402→    lastUserMessage.content,
   403→  );
   404→
   405→  let selectedCandidates;
   406→  if (queryEmbedding) {
   407→    // 5. Rank candidates by weighted cosine similarity
   408→    const ranked = rankCandidates(allCandidates, queryEmbedding);
   409→    // 6. Select within token budget
   410→    selectedCandidates = selectWithinBudget(ranked, intelligenceConfig.contextInjectionTokenBudget);
   411→  } else {
   412→    // No embedding available -- fall back to all candidates within budget
   413→    const ranked = allCandidates.map((c) => ({
   414→      id: c.id,
   415→      type: c.type,
   416→      text: c.text,
   417→      score: c.weight,
   418→    }));
   419→    selectedCandidates = selectWithinBudget(ranked, intelligenceConfig.contextInjectionTokenBudget);
   420→  }
   421→
   422→  if (selectedCandidates.length === 0) {
   423→    return { body: originalBody };
   424→  }
   425→
   426→  // 7. Build XML block
   427→  const brainContextXml = buildBrainContextXml(selectedCandidates);
   428→
   429→  // 8. Inject into system prompt
   430→  const injectionResult = injectBrainContext(parsedBody.system, brainContextXml);
   431→
   432→  // 9. Build modified request body
   433→  const modifiedBody = {
   434→    ...parsedBody,
   435→    system: injectionResult.system,
   436→  };
   437→
   438→  return {
   439→    body: JSON.stringify(modifiedBody),
   440→    injectionResult,
   441→  };
   442→}
   443→
   444→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   445→// Intelligence Metadata Builder (pure)
   446→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   447→
   448→function buildIntelligenceMetadata(injectionResult: InjectionResult) {
   449→  return {
   450→    brain_context_injected: injectionResult.injected,
   451→    brain_context_decisions: injectionResult.decisionsCount,
   452→    brain_context_learnings: injectionResult.learningsCount,
   453→    brain_context_observations: injectionResult.observationsCount,
   454→    brain_context_tokens_est: injectionResult.tokensEstimated,
   455→  };
   456→}
   457→
   458→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   459→// Non-Streaming Response Content Extraction (for trace output)
   460→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   461→
   462→type ResponseContent = {
   463→  content_blocks: Array<{ type: string; text?: string; id?: string; name?: string; input?: unknown }>;
   464→  stop_reason: string;
   465→  usage: {
   466→    input_tokens: number;
   467→    output_tokens: number;
   468→    cache_creation_tokens?: number;
   469→    cache_read_tokens?: number;
   470→  };
   471→};
   472→
   473→function extractResponseContent(responseBody: string): ResponseContent | undefined {
   474→  try {
   475→    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody);
   476→    if (!parsed.content || !parsed.usage) return undefined;
   477→
   478→    return {
   479→      content_blocks: parsed.content,
   480→      stop_reason: parsed.stop_reason ?? "end_turn",
   481→      usage: {
   482→        input_tokens: parsed.usage.input_tokens ?? 0,
   483→        output_tokens: parsed.usage.output_tokens ?? 0,
   484→        cache_creation_tokens: parsed.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens,
   485→        cache_read_tokens: parsed.usage.cache_read_input_tokens,
   486→      },
   487→    };
   488→  } catch {
   489→    return undefined;
   490→  }
   491→}
   492→
   493→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   494→// Policy Denial Response Builder
   495→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   496→
   497→function buildPolicyDenialResponse(
   498→  result: Exclude<ProxyPolicyResult, { decision: "allow" }>,
   499→): Response {
   500→  if (result.decision === "deny_rate_limit") {
   501→    return new Response(JSON.stringify(result.body), {
   502→      status: result.status,
   503→      headers: {
   504→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   505→        "Retry-After": String(result.retryAfterSeconds),
   506→        "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
   507→      },
   508→    });
   509→  }
   510→  return jsonResponse(result.body, result.status);
   511→}
   512→
   513→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   514→// Streaming Trace Builder
   515→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   516→
   517→function buildStreamingTraceData(
   518→  streamCtx: StreamContext,
   519→  latencyMs: number,
   520→  identitySignals: import("./identity-resolver").IdentitySignals,
   521→  effectiveSessionId: string | undefined,
   522→  policyDecision: PolicyDecisionLog | undefined,
   523→  conversationId: string | undefined,
   524→  injectionResult: InjectionResult | undefined,
   525→): TraceData {
   526→  return {
   527→    model: streamCtx.model!,
   528→    inputTokens: streamCtx.inputTokens,
   529→    outputTokens: streamCtx.outputTokens,
   530→    cacheCreationTokens: streamCtx.cacheCreationTokens,
   531→    cacheReadTokens: streamCtx.cacheReadTokens,
   532→    stopReason: streamCtx.stopReason,
   533→    latencyMs,
   534→    workspaceId: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   535→    sessionId: effectiveSessionId,
   536→    taskId: identitySignals.taskId,
   537→    policyDecision,
   538→    conversationId,
   539→    ...(injectionResult ? {
   540→      intelligenceMetadata: buildIntelligenceMetadata(injectionResult),
   541→    } : {}),
   542→  };
   543→}
   544→
   545→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   546→// Handler Factory
   547→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   548→
   549→export function createAnthropicProxyHandler(
   550→  deps: ServerDependencies,
   551→): (request: Request) => Promise<Response> {
   552→  const workspaceCache: WorkspaceCache = new Map();
   553→  const rateLimiterState: RateLimiterState = createRateLimiterState();
   554→  const spendCache: SpendCache = new Map();
   555→  const noPolicyWarnedWorkspaces = new Set<string>();
   556→  const contextCache: ContextCache = createContextCache(300); // default TTL, overridden per-workspace
   557→
   558→  // Periodic pruning of stale rate limiter entries to prevent unbounded Map growth.
   559→  // unref() ensures this interval does not keep the process alive on shutdown.
   560→  const pruneInterval = setInterval(
   561→    () => pruneStaleEntries(rateLimiterState, Date.now()),
   562→    5 * 60 * 1000,
   563→  );
   564→  pruneInterval.unref();
   565→
   566→  const anthropicApiUrl = deps.config.anthropicApiUrl;
   567→
   568→  return async (request: Request): Promise<Response> => {
   569→    const startedAt = performance.now();
   570→    const url = new URL(request.url);
   571→    const upstreamPath = url.pathname.replace(/^\/proxy\/llm\/anthropic/, "");
   572→    const upstreamUrl = `${anthropicApiUrl}${upstreamPath}`;
   573→    const isCountTokens = isCountTokensRequest(url.pathname);
   574→
   575→    // --- Step 1: Parse request body (malformed body forwarded as-is) ---
   576→    const body = await request.text();
   577→    const parsed = tryParseRequestBody(body);
   578→    const isStreaming = parsed?.stream === true;
   579→
   580→    // --- Step 2: Identity resolution ---
   581→    const identitySignals = resolveIdentity({
   582→      metadataUserId: parsed?.metadata?.user_id,
   583→      workspaceHeader: request.headers.get("X-Brain-Workspace") ?? undefined,
   584→      taskHeader: request.headers.get("X-Brain-Task") ?? undefined,
   585→      agentTypeHeader: request.headers.get("X-Brain-Agent-Type") ?? undefined,
   586→      sessionHeader: request.headers.get("X-Brain-Session") ?? undefined,
   587→    });
   588→
   589→    // --- Step 3: Session ID resolution ---
   590→    // resolveSessionId returns either the header PK or the external Claude Code
   591→    // session UUID. Resolve to the actual agent_session PK via DB lookup.
   592→    const rawSessionId = resolveSessionId(identitySignals);
   593→    const effectiveSessionId = rawSessionId
   594→      ? await resolveAgentSessionId(deps.surreal, rawSessionId)
   595→      : undefined;
   596→
   597→    // --- Step 3.5: Conversation hash resolution (pure) ---
   598→    const conversationHashInput: ConversationHashInput = {
   599→      systemPrompt: typeof parsed?.system === "string" ? parsed.system : undefined,
   600→      systemPromptBlocks: Array.isArray(parsed?.system) ? parsed.system : undefined,
   601→      messages: parsed?.messages ?? [],
   602→    };
   603→    const conversationHash = resolveConversationHash(conversationHashInput);
   604→
   605→    // --- Conversation upsert (async, non-blocking) ---
   606→    let conversationId: string | undefined;
   607→    if (conversationHash && identitySignals.workspaceId) {
   608→      try {
   609→        const conversationRecord = await upsertConversation(
   610→          {
   611→            conversationId: conversationHash.conversationId,
   612→            workspaceId: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   613→            title: conversationHash.title,
   614→          },
   615→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   616→        );
   617→        if (conversationRecord) {
   618→          conversationId = conversationHash.conversationId;
   619→        }
   620→      } catch (error) {
   621→        logWarn("proxy.anthropic.conversation_upsert_failed", "Conversation upsert failed — continuing without conversation link", {
   622→          error: String(error),
   623→        });
   624→      }
   625→    }
   626→
   627→    // --- Workspace validation (non-blocking) ---
   628→    if (identitySignals.workspaceId) {
   629→      const isValid = await validateWorkspace(
   630→        deps.surreal,
   631→        identitySignals.workspaceId,
   632→        workspaceCache,
   633→      );
   634→      if (!isValid) {
   635→        logWarn("proxy.anthropic.invalid_workspace", "Workspace not found in database", {
   636→          workspaceId: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   637→        });
   638→      }
   639→    }
   640→
   641→    // --- Step 4: Policy evaluation ---
   642→    let policyResult: ProxyPolicyResult | undefined;
   643→    if (parsed?.model && !isCountTokens) {
   644→      const policyDeps: ProxyPolicyDependencies = {
   645→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   646→        inflight: deps.inflight,
   647→        rateLimiterState,
   648→        spendCache,
   649→      };
   650→
   651→      policyResult = await evaluateProxyPolicy(
   652→        {
   653→          workspaceId: identitySignals.workspaceId ?? "",
   654→          agentType: identitySignals.agentType,
   655→          model: parsed.model,
   656→        },
   657→        policyDeps,
   658→        noPolicyWarnedWorkspaces,
   659→      );
   660→
   661→      if (policyResult.decision !== "allow") {
   662→        return buildPolicyDenialResponse(policyResult);
   663→      }
   664→    }
   665→
   666→    // --- Step 5: Context injection (fail-open) ---
   667→    let effectiveBody = body;
   668→    let injectionResult: InjectionResult | undefined;
   669→
   670→    if (parsed && identitySignals.workspaceId && !isCountTokens) {
   671→      try {
   672→        const intelligenceConfig = await loadIntelligenceConfig(deps.surreal, identitySignals.workspaceId);
   673→        const contextResult = await runContextInjection(
   674→          deps,
   675→          identitySignals.workspaceId,
   676→          parsed,
   677→          body,
   678→          contextCache,
   679→          intelligenceConfig,
   680→        );
   681→        effectiveBody = contextResult.body;
   682→        injectionResult = contextResult.injectionResult;
   683→        if (intelligenceConfig.contextInjectionEnabled) {
   684→          logInfo("proxy.context_injection.result", "Context injection completed", {
   685→            workspace_id: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   686→            injected: injectionResult?.injected ?? false,
   687→            decisions: injectionResult?.decisionsCount ?? 0,
   688→            learnings: injectionResult?.learningsCount ?? 0,
   689→            observations: injectionResult?.observationsCount ?? 0,
   690→          });
   691→        }
   692→      } catch (error) {
   693→        // Fail-open: log warning and continue with original body
   694→        logWarn("proxy.context_injection.failed", "Context injection failed, forwarding original request", {
   695→          workspace_id: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   696→          error: String(error),
   697→        });
   698→      }
   699→    }
   700→
   701→    // --- API key validation ---
   702→    const hasApiKey = request.headers.has("x-api-key");
   703→    const hasAuthHeader = request.headers.has("authorization");
   704→    if (!hasApiKey && !hasAuthHeader) {
   705→      return jsonResponse(
   706→        { error: "unauthorized", message: "Missing x-api-key or authorization header" },
   707→        401,
   708→      );
   709→    }
   710→
   711→    // --- Build identity context for logging ---
   712→    const identityContext = {
   713→      user_hash: identitySignals.userHash,
   714→      account_id: identitySignals.accountId,
   715→      session_id: effectiveSessionId,
   716→      workspace_id: identitySignals.workspaceId,
   717→      task_id: identitySignals.taskId,
   718→      agent_type: identitySignals.agentType,
   719→      is_count_tokens: isCountTokens || undefined,
   720→    };
   721→
   722→    logInfo("proxy.anthropic.request", "Forwarding to Anthropic", {
   723→      method: request.method,
   724→      url: upstreamUrl,
   725→      ...identityContext,
   726→    });
   727→
   728→    // --- Build policy decision for audit trail ---
   729→    const policyDecision: PolicyDecisionLog | undefined = policyResult
   730→      ? {
   731→          decision: "pass",
   732→          policy_refs: policyResult.decision === "allow" ? policyResult.policyIds : [],
   733→          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   734→        }
   735→      : undefined;
   736→
   737→    // --- Step 6: Request forwarding ---
   738→    const upstreamHeaders = buildUpstreamHeaders(request);
   739→
   740→    let upstream: Response;
   741→    try {
   742→      upstream = await fetch(upstreamUrl, {
   743→        method: request.method,
   744→        headers: upstreamHeaders,
   745→        body: request.method !== "GET" ? effectiveBody : undefined,
   746→      });
   747→    } catch (error) {
   748→      logError("proxy.anthropic.upstream_error", "Failed to reach Anthropic API", error);
   749→      return jsonResponse({ error: "upstream_unreachable", source: "proxy" }, 502);
   750→    }
   751→
   752→    // --- Non-streaming response ---
   753→    if (!isStreaming) {
   754→      const responseBody = await upstream.text();
   755→      const latencyMs = elapsedMs(startedAt);
   756→
   757→      logInfo("proxy.anthropic.response", "Anthropic response", {
   758→        status: upstream.status,
   759→        latency_ms: latencyMs,
   760→        ...identityContext,
   761→      });
   762→
   763→      // Async trace capture for non-streaming (skip count_tokens)
   764→      if (!isCountTokens && upstream.status >= 200 && upstream.status < 300) {
   765→        const traceData = extractNonStreamingUsage(responseBody, parsed?.model, latencyMs, identitySignals, effectiveSessionId, policyDecision, conversationId, injectionResult);
   766→        if (traceData) {
   767→          deps.inflight.track(
   768→            captureTrace(traceData, { surreal: deps.surreal }).catch(() => undefined),
   769→          );
   770→        }
   771→      }
   772→
   773→      return new Response(responseBody, {
   774→        status: upstream.status,
   775→        headers: {
   776→          "Content-Type": upstream.headers.get("content-type") ?? "application/json",
   777→          "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
   778→        },
   779→      });
   780→    }
   781→
   782→    // --- Streaming: pipe SSE events through ---
   783→    const { readable, writable } = new TransformStream<Uint8Array>();
   784→    const writer = writable.getWriter();
   785→    const reader = upstream.body!.getReader();
   786→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
   787→
   788→    const streamContext: StreamContext = {
   789→      model: parsed?.model,
   790→      inputTokens: 0,
   791→      outputTokens: 0,
   792→      cacheCreationTokens: 0,
   793→      cacheReadTokens: 0,
   794→      stopReason: undefined,
   795→    };
   796→
   797→    (async () => {
   798→      try {
   799→        let buffer = "";
   800→        while (true) {
   801→          const { done, value } = await reader.read();
   802→          if (done) break;
   803→
   804→          await writer.write(value);
   805→
   806→          buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
   807→          buffer = extractSSEUsage(buffer, streamContext);
   808→        }
   809→      } catch (error) {
   810→        logError("proxy.anthropic.stream_error", "SSE relay error", error);
   811→      } finally {
   812→        await writer.close();
   813→
   814→        const latencyMs = elapsedMs(startedAt);
   815→
   816→        logInfo("proxy.anthropic.response", "Anthropic stream complete", {
   817→          model: streamContext.model,
   818→          input_tokens: streamContext.inputTokens,
   819→          output_tokens: streamContext.outputTokens,
   820→          cache_creation_tokens: streamContext.cacheCreationTokens,
   821→          cache_read_tokens: streamContext.cacheReadTokens,
   822→          stop_reason: streamContext.stopReason,
   823→          latency_ms: latencyMs,
   824→          ...identityContext,
   825→        });
   826→
   827→        // Async trace capture for streaming (skip count_tokens)
   828→        if (!isCountTokens && streamContext.model) {
   829→          const traceData = buildStreamingTraceData(
   830→            streamContext, latencyMs, identitySignals,
   831→            effectiveSessionId, policyDecision, conversationId, injectionResult,
   832→          );
   833→          deps.inflight.track(
   834→            captureTrace(traceData, { surreal: deps.surreal }).catch(() => undefined),
   835→          );
   836→        }
   837→      }
   838→    })();
   839→
   840→    return new Response(readable, {
   841→      status: upstream.status,
   842→      headers: {
   843→        "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
   844→        "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
   845→        "Connection": "keep-alive",
   846→        "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
   847→        "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
   848→      },
   849→    });
   850→  };
   851→}
   852→
```
