     1→/**
     2→ * Acceptance Tests: Conversation Hash Correlation (ADR-050)
     3→ *
     4→ * Traces: Intelligence Capability — Conversation Hash Correlation
     5→ * Driving port: POST /proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages + SurrealDB graph queries
     6→ *
     7→ * Validates that the proxy derives a deterministic conversation identity from
     8→ * request content (system prompt + first user message) using UUIDv5, enabling
     9→ * trace grouping without requiring Brain integration or session lifecycle.
    10→ *
    11→ * Implementation sequence:
    12→ * 1. Walking skeleton: same conversation content produces same conversation record
    13→ * 2. Different content produces different conversation record
    14→ * 3. Conversation record has correct workspace and title
    15→ * 4. Missing system prompt — trace created without conversation link
    16→ * 5. Missing first user message — trace created without conversation link
    17→ * 6. Multiple turns in same conversation share the same conversation ID
    18→ *
    19→ * Tests enabled — implementing conversation hash correlation.
    20→ */
    21→import { describe, expect, it } from "bun:test";
    22→import {
    23→  setupAcceptanceSuite,
    24→  sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence,
    25→  createProxyTestWorkspace,
    26→  getTracesForWorkspace,
    27→  getConversationsForWorkspace,
    28→} from "./llm-proxy-test-kit";
    29→
    30→const getRuntime = setupAcceptanceSuite("llm_proxy_conversation_hash");
    31→
    32→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→// Walking Skeleton: Same conversation content links to same conversation record
    34→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→describe("Walking Skeleton: Identical requests grouped into same conversation", () => {
    36→  it("creates a single conversation record when two requests share the same system prompt and first user message", async () => {
    37→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    38→
    39→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-skel-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    40→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
    41→
    42→    const systemPrompt = "You are a TypeScript expert.";
    43→    const firstUserMessage = "How do I implement dependency injection?";
    44→
    45→    // Given two requests with the same system prompt and first user message
    46→    const response1 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
    47→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
    48→      stream: false,
    49→      maxTokens: 50,
    50→      messages: [{ role: "user", content: firstUserMessage }],
    51→      systemPrompt,
    52→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
    53→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
    54→    });
    55→    expect(response1.status).toBe(200);
    56→    await response1.json();
    57→
    58→    const response2 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
    59→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
    60→      stream: false,
    61→      maxTokens: 50,
    62→      messages: [
    63→        { role: "user", content: firstUserMessage },
    64→        { role: "assistant", content: "Here is how..." },
    65→        { role: "user", content: "Can you show an example?" },
    66→      ],
    67→      systemPrompt,
    68→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
    69→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
    70→    });
    71→    expect(response2.status).toBe(200);
    72→    await response2.json();
    73→
    74→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
    75→
    76→    // Then both traces link to the same conversation record
    77→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
    78→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(1);
    79→
    80→    // And the conversation has the expected workspace
    81→    expect((conversations[0].workspace.id as string)).toBe(workspaceId);
    82→  }, 60_000);
    83→});
    84→
    85→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    86→// Focused Scenarios
    87→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    88→
    89→describe("Different content produces different conversation record", () => {
    90→  it("creates separate conversation records for requests with different system prompts", async () => {
    91→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
    92→
    93→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-diff-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    94→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
    95→
    96→    // Given a request with one system prompt
    97→    const response1 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
    98→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
    99→      stream: false,
   100→      maxTokens: 50,
   101→      messages: [{ role: "user", content: "Help me write code" }],
   102→      systemPrompt: "You are a Python developer.",
   103→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
   104→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
   105→    });
   106→    expect(response1.status).toBe(200);
   107→    await response1.json();
   108→
   109→    // And a request with a different system prompt
   110→    const response2 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
   111→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
   112→      stream: false,
   113→      maxTokens: 50,
   114→      messages: [{ role: "user", content: "Help me write code" }],
   115→      systemPrompt: "You are a Rust developer.",
   116→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
   117→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
   118→    });
   119→    expect(response2.status).toBe(200);
   120→    await response2.json();
   121→
   122→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
   123→
   124→    // Then two separate conversation records exist
   125→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
   126→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(2);
   127→  }, 60_000);
   128→});
   129→
   130→describe("Conversation record has correct title derived from first user message", () => {
   131→  it("sets the conversation title from the first user message content", async () => {
   132→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
   133→
   134→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-title-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   135→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
   136→
   137→    const firstMessage = "How do I implement OAuth 2.1 with DPoP?";
   138→
   139→    // When a request is sent with a specific first user message

   141→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
   142→      stream: false,
   143→      maxTokens: 50,
   144→      messages: [{ role: "user", content: firstMessage }],
   145→      systemPrompt: "You are a security expert.",
   146→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
   147→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
   148→    });
   149→    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
   150→    await response.json();
   151→
   152→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
   153→
   154→    // Then the conversation title is derived from the first user message
   155→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
   156→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(1);
   157→    expect(conversations[0].title).toContain("OAuth");
   158→  }, 30_000);
   159→});
   160→
   161→describe("Missing system prompt — trace created without conversation link", () => {
   162→  it("creates a trace but skips conversation hash when no system prompt is provided", async () => {
   163→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
   164→
   165→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-nosys-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   166→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
   167→
   168→    // Given a request with no system prompt
   169→    const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
   170→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
   171→      stream: false,
   172→      maxTokens: 50,
   173→      messages: [{ role: "user", content: "Hello" }],
   174→      // No systemPrompt
   175→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
   176→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
   177→    });
   178→
   179→    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
   180→    await response.json();
   181→
   182→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
   183→
   184→    // Then a trace exists but no conversation record was created
   185→    const traces = await getTracesForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
   186→    expect(traces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
   187→
   188→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
   189→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(0);
   190→  }, 30_000);
   191→});
   192→
   193→describe("Missing first user message — trace created without conversation link", () => {
   194→  it("creates a trace but skips conversation hash when messages array is empty", async () => {
   195→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
   196→
   197→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-nomsg-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   198→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
   199→
   200→    // Given a request with system prompt but no user messages
   201→    // (This is technically an invalid Anthropic request that will fail upstream,
   202→    // but the proxy should still attempt trace creation without conversation link)
   203→    const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
   204→      model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",
   205→      stream: false,
   206→      maxTokens: 50,
   207→      messages: [], // Empty messages
   208→      systemPrompt: "You are a helpful assistant.",
   209→      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
   210→      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,
   211→    });
   212→
   213→    // The upstream may reject this, but we verify the proxy's behavior
   214→    // regardless of upstream response
   215→    await response.text(); // consume body
   216→
   217→    // No conversation should be created for missing first user message
   218→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
   219→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(0);
   220→  }, 30_000);
   221→});
   222→
   223→describe("Multiple turns preserve same conversation identity", () => {
   224→  it("links all traces in a multi-turn conversation to the same conversation record", async () => {
   225→    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();
   226→
   227→    const workspaceId = `ws-conv-multi-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   228→    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);

   230→    const systemPrompt = "You are an architect.";

   233→    // Given three turns in the same conversation (same system + first user message)
   234→    for (let turn = 0; turn < 3; turn++) {
   235→      const messages = [
   236→        { role: "user", content: firstMessage },
   237→      ];
   238→      // Add previous turns for subsequent requests
   239→      for (let i = 0; i < turn; i++) {

   241→        messages.push({ role: "user", content: `Follow-up ${i}` });
   242→      }
   243→
   244→      const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {
   245→        model: "claude-sonnet-4-20250514",

   247→        maxTokens: 30,
   248→        messages,
   249→        systemPrompt,
   250→        apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,

   252→      });
   253→      expect(response.status).toBe(200);
   254→      await response.json();
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 3000));
   258→
   259→    // Then all three traces link to the same single conversation
   260→    const conversations = await getConversationsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: "proxy" });
   261→    expect(conversations.length).toBe(1);
   262→
   263→    const traces = await getTracesForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);
   264→    expect(traces.length).toBe(3);
   265→  }, 90_000);
   266→});

     1→/**
     2→ * LLM Proxy Acceptance Test Kit
     3→ *
     4→ * Domain-specific helpers for LLM proxy acceptance tests.
     5→ * Extends the shared acceptance-test-kit with proxy-specific
     6→ * workspace setup, request builders, and graph query helpers.
     7→ *
     8→ * Driving ports: HTTP endpoints at /proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages
     9→ * and /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/proxy/spend
    10→ */
    11→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    12→import {
    13→  setupAcceptanceSuite,
    14→  createTestUser,
    15→  fetchRaw,
    16→  type AcceptanceTestRuntime,
    17→  type TestUser,
    18→} from "../acceptance-test-kit";
    19→
    20→// Re-export shared helpers
    21→export {
    22→  setupAcceptanceSuite,
    23→  createTestUser,
    24→  fetchRaw,
    25→  type AcceptanceTestRuntime,
    26→  type TestUser,
    27→};
    28→
    29→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→// Proxy Request Builders
    31→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→export type ProxyRequestOptions = {
    34→  model: string;
    35→  stream?: boolean;
    36→  maxTokens?: number;
    37→  messages?: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>;
    38→  metadata?: { user_id?: string };
    39→  apiKey?: string;
    40→  workspaceHeader?: string;
    41→  taskHeader?: string;
    42→  agentTypeHeader?: string;
    43→};
    44→
    45→/**
    46→ * Build a proxy request body matching the Anthropic Messages API format.
    47→ */
    48→export function buildProxyRequestBody(options: ProxyRequestOptions): string {
    49→  return JSON.stringify({
    50→    model: options.model,
    51→    max_tokens: options.maxTokens ?? 100,
    52→    stream: options.stream ?? false,
    53→    messages: options.messages ?? [
    54→      { role: "user", content: "Say hello in one word." },
    55→    ],
    56→    ...(options.metadata ? { metadata: options.metadata } : {}),
    57→  });
    58→}
    59→
    60→/**
    61→ * Build headers for a proxy request, including identity headers.
    62→ */
    63→export function buildProxyHeaders(options: ProxyRequestOptions): Record<string, string> {
    64→  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    65→    "Content-Type": "application/json",
    66→    "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
    67→  };
    68→
    69→  if (options.apiKey) {
    70→    headers["x-api-key"] = options.apiKey;
    71→  }
    72→  if (options.workspaceHeader) {
    73→    headers["X-Brain-Workspace"] = options.workspaceHeader;
    74→  }
    75→  if (options.taskHeader) {
    76→    headers["X-Brain-Task"] = options.taskHeader;
    77→  }
    78→  if (options.agentTypeHeader) {
    79→    headers["X-Brain-Agent-Type"] = options.agentTypeHeader;
    80→  }
    81→
    82→  return headers;
    83→}
    84→
    85→/**
    86→ * Send a request through the LLM proxy endpoint.
    87→ * Returns the raw Response for flexible assertion.
    88→ */
    89→export async function sendProxyRequest(
    90→  baseUrl: string,
    91→  options: ProxyRequestOptions,
    92→): Promise<Response> {
    93→  const body = buildProxyRequestBody(options);
    94→  const headers = buildProxyHeaders(options);
    95→
    96→  return fetch(`${baseUrl}/proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages`, {
    97→    method: "POST",
    98→    headers,
    99→    body,
   100→  });
   101→}
   102→
   103→/**
   104→ * Send a count_tokens request through the proxy.
   105→ */
   106→export async function sendCountTokensRequest(
   107→  baseUrl: string,
   108→  options: ProxyRequestOptions,
   109→): Promise<Response> {
   110→  const body = buildProxyRequestBody(options);
   111→  const headers = buildProxyHeaders(options);
   112→
   113→  return fetch(`${baseUrl}/proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages/count_tokens`, {
   114→    method: "POST",
   115→    headers,
   116→    body,
   117→  });
   118→}
   119→
   120→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→// SSE Stream Helpers
   122→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→export type SSEEvent = {
   125→  event?: string;
   126→  data: string;
   127→};
   128→
   129→/**
   130→ * Collect all SSE events from a streaming proxy response.
   131→ */
   132→export async function collectProxySSEEvents(response: Response): Promise<SSEEvent[]> {
   133→  if (!response.body) {
   134→    throw new Error("No response body for SSE collection");
   135→  }
   136→
   137→  const reader = response.body.getReader();
   138→  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
   139→  const events: SSEEvent[] = [];
   140→  let buffer = "";
   141→
   142→  try {
   143→    while (true) {
   144→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
   145→      if (done) break;
   146→
   147→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
   148→      const segments = buffer.split("\n\n");
   149→      buffer = segments.pop() ?? "";
   150→
   151→      for (const segment of segments) {
   152→        const lines = segment.split("\n");
   153→        let eventType: string | undefined;
   154→        let dataLine: string | undefined;
   155→
   156→        for (const line of lines) {
   157→          if (line.startsWith("event: ")) {
   158→            eventType = line.slice(7);
   159→          } else if (line.startsWith("data: ")) {
   160→            dataLine = line.slice(6);
   161→          }
   162→        }
   163→
   164→        if (dataLine !== undefined) {
   165→          events.push({ event: eventType, data: dataLine });
   166→        }
   167→      }
   168→    }
   169→  } finally {
   170→    reader.releaseLock();
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  return events;
   174→}
   175→
   176→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   177→// Workspace and Identity Setup
   178→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   179→
   180→/**
   181→ * Create a workspace record in SurrealDB for proxy testing.
   182→ */
   183→export async function createProxyTestWorkspace(
   184→  surreal: Surreal,
   185→  workspaceId: string,
   186→  options?: { dailyBudget?: number; alertThreshold?: number },
   187→): Promise<string> {
   188→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   189→
   190→  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {
   191→    name: `Proxy Test Workspace ${workspaceId}`,
   192→    status: "active",
   193→    onboarding_complete: true,
   194→    onboarding_turn_count: 0,
   195→    onboarding_summary_pending: false,
   196→    onboarding_started_at: new Date(),
   197→    created_at: new Date(),
   198→  };
   199→
   200→  if (options?.dailyBudget !== undefined) {
   201→    content.daily_budget_usd = options.dailyBudget;
   202→  }
   203→
   204→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $workspace CONTENT $content;`, {
   205→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
   206→    content,
   207→  });
   208→
   209→  return workspaceId;
   210→}
   211→
   212→/**
   213→ * Create a project record linked to a workspace.
   214→ */
   215→export async function createProxyTestProject(
   216→  surreal: Surreal,
   217→  projectId: string,
   218→  workspaceId: string,
   219→): Promise<string> {
   220→  const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", projectId);
   221→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   222→
   223→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $project CONTENT $content;`, {
   224→    project: projectRecord,
   225→    content: {
   226→      name: `Test Project ${projectId}`,
   227→      status: "active",
   228→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   229→      created_at: new Date(),
   230→    },
   231→  });
   232→
   233→  return projectId;
   234→}
   235→
   236→/**
   237→ * Create a task record linked to a project and workspace.
   238→ */
   239→export async function createProxyTestTask(
   240→  surreal: Surreal,
   241→  taskId: string,
   242→  projectId: string,
   243→  workspaceId: string,
   244→): Promise<string> {
   245→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
   246→  const projectRecord = new RecordId("project", projectId);
   247→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   248→
   249→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $task CONTENT $content;`, {
   250→    task: taskRecord,
   251→    content: {
   252→      title: `Test Task ${taskId}`,
   253→      status: "open",
   254→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   255→      created_at: new Date(),
   256→    },
   257→  });
   258→
   259→  // Link task to project
   260→  await surreal.query(`RELATE $task->belongs_to->$project SET added_at = time::now();`, {
   261→    task: taskRecord,
   262→    project: projectRecord,
   263→  });
   264→
   265→  return taskId;
   266→}
   267→
   268→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   269→// Graph Query Helpers
   270→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   271→
   272→export type LlmTraceRecord = {
   273→  id: RecordId;
   274→  model: string;
   275→  input_tokens: number;
   276→  output_tokens: number;
   277→  cache_creation_tokens?: number;
   278→  cache_read_tokens?: number;
   279→  cost_usd: number;
   280→  latency_ms: number;
   281→  stop_reason?: string;
   282→  request_id?: string;
   283→  created_at: Date;
   284→};
   285→
   286→/**
   287→ * Query all LLM traces in a workspace.
   288→ */
   289→export async function getTracesForWorkspace(
   290→  surreal: Surreal,
   291→  workspaceId: string,
   292→): Promise<LlmTraceRecord[]> {
   293→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   294→
   295→  const results = await surreal.query(
   296→    `SELECT * FROM trace WHERE ->scoped_to->workspace CONTAINS $ws ORDER BY created_at DESC;`,
   297→    { ws: workspaceRecord },
   298→  );
   299→
   300→  return (results[0] ?? []) as LlmTraceRecord[];
   301→}
   302→
   303→/**
   304→ * Query traces attributed to a specific task.
   305→ */
   306→export async function getTracesForTask(
   307→  surreal: Surreal,
   308→  taskId: string,
   309→): Promise<LlmTraceRecord[]> {
   310→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
   311→
   312→  const results = await surreal.query(
   313→    `SELECT * FROM trace WHERE ->attributed_to->task CONTAINS $task ORDER BY created_at DESC;`,
   314→    { task: taskRecord },
   315→  );
   316→
   317→  return (results[0] ?? []) as LlmTraceRecord[];
   318→}
   319→
   320→/**
   321→ * Check whether a trace has the expected graph edges.
   322→ */
   323→export async function getTraceEdges(
   324→  surreal: Surreal,
   325→  traceId: string,
   326→): Promise<{
   327→  workspaces: RecordId[];
   328→  tasks: RecordId[];
   329→  sessions: RecordId[];
   330→}> {
   331→  const traceRecord = new RecordId("trace", traceId);
   332→
   333→  const results = await surreal.query(
   334→    `SELECT
   335→       ->scoped_to->workspace AS workspaces,
   336→       ->attributed_to->task AS tasks,
   337→       <-invoked<-agent_session AS sessions
   338→     FROM $trace;`,
   339→    { trace: traceRecord },
   340→  );
   341→
   342→  const row = (results[0] as Array<{
   343→    workspaces: RecordId[];
   344→    tasks: RecordId[];
   345→    sessions: RecordId[];
   346→  }>)?.[0];
   347→
   348→  return {
   349→    workspaces: row?.workspaces ?? [],
   350→    tasks: row?.tasks ?? [],
   351→    sessions: row?.sessions ?? [],
   352→  };
   353→}
   354→
   355→/**
   356→ * Query the total spend for a workspace (sum of trace costs).
   357→ */
   358→export async function getWorkspaceSpend(
   359→  surreal: Surreal,
   360→  workspaceId: string,
   361→): Promise<number> {
   362→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   363→
   364→  const results = await surreal.query(
   365→    `SELECT cost_usd FROM trace WHERE workspace = $ws;`,
   366→    { ws: workspaceRecord },
   367→  );
   368→
   369→  const rows = (results[0] ?? []) as Array<{ cost_usd: number }>;
   370→  return rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (row.cost_usd ?? 0), 0);
   371→}
   372→
   373→/**
   374→ * Seed an LLM trace directly for testing dashboard/audit scenarios
   375→ * that need pre-existing trace data.
   376→ */
   377→export async function seedLlmTrace(
   378→  surreal: Surreal,
   379→  traceId: string,
   380→  options: {
   381→    model: string;
   382→    input_tokens: number;
   383→    output_tokens: number;
   384→    cost_usd: number;
   385→    latency_ms: number;
   386→    workspaceId: string;
   387→    taskId?: string;
   388→    sessionId?: string;
   389→    stop_reason?: string;
   390→    cache_read_tokens?: number;
   391→    cache_creation_tokens?: number;
   392→    created_at?: Date;
   393→  },
   394→): Promise<string> {
   395→  const traceRecord = new RecordId("trace", traceId);
   396→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", options.workspaceId);
   397→
   398→  // Ensure a test identity exists for the actor field (required by trace schema)
   399→  const actorId = `proxy-test-actor-${options.workspaceId}`;
   400→  const actorRecord = new RecordId("identity", actorId);
   401→  await surreal.query(
   402→    `CREATE $actor CONTENT { name: "proxy-test-system", type: "system", workspace: $ws, created_at: time::now() };`,
   403→    { actor: actorRecord, ws: workspaceRecord },
   404→  ).catch(() => undefined); // Ignore if already exists
   405→
   406→  const traceContent: Record<string, unknown> = {
   407→    type: "llm_call",
   408→    model: options.model,
   409→    input_tokens: options.input_tokens,
   410→    output_tokens: options.output_tokens,
   411→    cache_read_tokens: options.cache_read_tokens ?? 0,
   412→    cache_creation_tokens: options.cache_creation_tokens ?? 0,
   413→    cost_usd: options.cost_usd,
   414→    latency_ms: options.latency_ms,
   415→    stop_reason: options.stop_reason ?? "end_turn",
   416→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
   417→    actor: actorRecord,
   418→    created_at: options.created_at ?? new Date(),
   419→  };
   420→
   421→  // Link session directly on trace record (schema: session TYPE option<record<agent_session>>)
   422→  if (options.sessionId) {
   423→    traceContent.session = new RecordId("agent_session", options.sessionId);
   424→  }
   425→
   426→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $trace CONTENT $content;`, {
   427→    trace: traceRecord,
   428→    content: traceContent,
   429→  });
   430→
   431→  // Create workspace edge
   432→  await surreal.query(
   433→    `RELATE $trace->scoped_to->$workspace SET created_at = time::now();`,
   434→    { trace: traceRecord, workspace: workspaceRecord },
   435→  );
   436→
   437→  // Create task edge if provided
   438→  if (options.taskId) {
   439→    const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", options.taskId);
   440→    await surreal.query(
   441→      `RELATE $trace->attributed_to->$task SET created_at = time::now();`,
   442→      { trace: traceRecord, task: taskRecord },
   443→    );
   444→  }
   445→
   446→  // Create session edge if provided
   447→  if (options.sessionId) {
   448→    const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", options.sessionId);
   449→    await surreal.query(
   450→      `RELATE $sess->invoked->$trace SET created_at = time::now();`,
   451→      { trace: traceRecord, sess: sessionRecord },
   452→    );
   453→  }
   454→
   455→  return traceId;
   456→}

   462→/**
   463→ * Create a model access policy for the proxy.
   464→ */
   465→export async function createModelAccessPolicy(
   466→  surreal: Surreal,
   467→  workspaceId: string,
   468→  options: {
   469→    policyId: string;
   470→    agentType: string;
   471→    allowedModels: string[];
   472→    status?: string;
   473→  },
   474→): Promise<string> {
   475→  const policyRecord = new RecordId("policy", options.policyId);
   476→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   477→
   478→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $policy CONTENT $content;`, {
   479→    policy: policyRecord,
   480→    content: {
   481→      title: `Model Access: ${options.agentType}`,
   482→      description: `Controls which models ${options.agentType} can use`,
   483→      status: options.status ?? "active",
   484→      version: 1,
   485→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   486→      rules: [{
   487→        id: "model_access",
   488→        condition: { field: "agent_type", operator: "eq", value: options.agentType },
   489→        effect: "allow",
   490→        priority: 50,
   491→        params: { allowed_models: options.allowedModels },
   492→      }],
   493→      created_at: new Date(),
   494→    },
   495→  });
   496→
   497→  return options.policyId;
   498→}

   598→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   599→// Intelligence: Proxy Intelligence Config
   600→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   601→
   602→/**
   603→ * Create a proxy_intelligence_config record for a workspace.
   604→ * Controls context injection, contradiction detection, and session timeout.
   605→ */
   606→export async function createProxyIntelligenceConfig(
   607→  surreal: Surreal,
   608→  workspaceId: string,
   609→  options?: {
   610→    contextInjectionEnabled?: boolean;
   611→    contextInjectionTokenBudget?: number;
   612→    contextInjectionCacheTtlSeconds?: number;
   613→    contextInjectionTier?: "fast" | "secure";
   614→    contradictionDetectionEnabled?: boolean;
   615→    contradictionTier1Threshold?: number;
   616→    contradictionTier2ConfidenceMin?: number;
   617→  },
   618→): Promise<string> {
   619→  const configId = `cfg-${workspaceId}`;
   620→  const configRecord = new RecordId("proxy_intelligence_config", configId);
   621→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   622→
   623→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $config CONTENT $content;`, {
   624→    config: configRecord,
   625→    content: {
   626→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   627→      context_injection_enabled: options?.contextInjectionEnabled ?? true,
   628→      context_injection_token_budget: options?.contextInjectionTokenBudget ?? 1000,
   629→      context_injection_cache_ttl_seconds: options?.contextInjectionCacheTtlSeconds ?? 300,
   630→      context_injection_tier: options?.contextInjectionTier ?? "secure",
   631→      contradiction_detection_enabled: options?.contradictionDetectionEnabled ?? true,
   632→      contradiction_tier1_threshold: options?.contradictionTier1Threshold ?? 0.75,
   633→      contradiction_tier2_confidence_min: options?.contradictionTier2ConfidenceMin ?? 0.6,
   634→      created_at: new Date(),
   635→      updated_at: new Date(),
   636→    },
   637→  });
   638→
   639→  return configId;
   640→}

   646→export type AgentSessionRecord = {

   657→/**
   658→ * Query agent sessions for a workspace, optionally filtered by source.
   659→ */
   660→export async function getSessionsForWorkspace(
   661→  surreal: Surreal,
   662→  workspaceId: string,
   663→  options?: { source?: string },
   664→): Promise<AgentSessionRecord[]> {
   665→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   666→
   667→  const sourceFilter = options?.source
   668→    ? `AND source = $source`
   669→    : "";
   670→
   671→  const results = await surreal.query(
   672→    `SELECT * FROM agent_session WHERE workspace = $ws ${sourceFilter} ORDER BY started_at DESC;`,
   673→    { ws: workspaceRecord, source: options?.source },
   674→  );
   675→
   676→  return (results[0] ?? []) as AgentSessionRecord[];
   677→}

   679→/**

   688→  const results = await surreal.query(

   696→/**
   697→ * Seed an agent session directly for testing scenarios
   698→ * that need pre-existing session data.
   699→ */
   700→export async function seedAgentSession(
   701→  surreal: Surreal,
   702→  sessionId: string,
   703→  options: {
   704→    workspaceId: string;
   705→    agent?: string;
   706→    source?: string;
   707→    externalSessionId?: string;
   708→    startedAt?: Date;
   709→    lastActivityAt?: Date;
   710→    endedAt?: Date;
   711→  },
   712→): Promise<string> {
   713→  const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", sessionId);
   714→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", options.workspaceId);
   715→
   716→  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {
   717→    agent: options.agent ?? "coding-agent",
   718→    workspace: workspaceRecord,
   719→    started_at: options.startedAt ?? new Date(),
   720→    last_event_at: options.lastActivityAt ?? new Date(),
   721→    created_at: new Date(),
   722→  };
   723→
   724→  if (options.externalSessionId) {
   725→    content.external_session_id = options.externalSessionId;
   726→  }
   727→  if (options.endedAt) {
   728→    content.ended_at = options.endedAt;
   729→  }
   730→  if (options.source) {
   731→    content.external_session_id = content.external_session_id ?? options.source;
   732→  }
   733→
   734→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $sess CONTENT $content;`, {
   735→    sess: sessionRecord,
   736→    content,
   737→  });
   738→
   739→  return sessionId;
   740→}
   741→
   742→/**
   743→ * Simulate a session becoming stale by backdating last_activity_at.
   744→ * This allows testing the background sweep timeout behavior.
   745→ */
   746→export async function simulateSessionTimeout(
   747→  surreal: Surreal,
   748→  sessionId: string,
   749→  minutesAgo: number,
   750→): Promise<void> {
   751→  const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", sessionId);
   752→  const staleTime = new Date(Date.now() - minutesAgo * 60 * 1000);
   753→
   754→  await surreal.query(
   755→    `UPDATE $sess SET last_activity_at = $staleTime;`,
   756→    { sess: sessionRecord, staleTime },
   757→  );
   758→}

   880→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   881→// Intelligence: Observation Query Helpers
   882→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   883→
   884→export type ObservationRecord = {
   885→  id: RecordId;
   886→  text: string;
   887→  severity: string;
   888→  status: string;
   889→  observation_type?: string;
   890→  source_agent?: string;
   891→  workspace: RecordId;
   892→  created_at: Date;
   893→};
   894→
   895→/**
   896→ * Query observations for a workspace, optionally filtered by type and source.
   897→ */
   898→export async function getObservationsForWorkspace(
   899→  surreal: Surreal,
   900→  workspaceId: string,
   901→  options?: { observationType?: string; sourceAgent?: string },
   902→): Promise<ObservationRecord[]> {
   903→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   904→
   905→  let filters = `WHERE workspace = $ws`;
   906→  if (options?.observationType) {
   907→    filters += ` AND observation_type = $obsType`;
   908→  }
   909→  if (options?.sourceAgent) {
   910→    filters += ` AND source_agent = $srcAgent`;
   911→  }
   912→
   913→  const results = await surreal.query(
   914→    `SELECT * FROM observation ${filters} ORDER BY created_at DESC;`,
   915→    {
   916→      ws: workspaceRecord,
   917→      obsType: options?.observationType,
   918→      srcAgent: options?.sourceAgent,
   919→    },
   920→  );
   921→
   922→  return (results[0] ?? []) as ObservationRecord[];
   923→}
   924→
   925→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   926→// Intelligence: Extended Proxy Request Options
   927→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   928→
   929→/**
   930→ * Send a proxy request with additional intelligence-related headers.
   931→ * Extends the base sendProxyRequest with session and session-end headers.
   932→ */
   933→export async function sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(
   934→  baseUrl: string,
   935→  options: ProxyRequestOptions & {
   936→    sessionHeader?: string;
   937→    sessionEndHeader?: boolean;
   938→    systemPrompt?: string | Array<{ type: string; text: string; cache_control?: { type: string } }>;
   939→  },
   940→): Promise<Response> {
   941→  const body = JSON.parse(buildProxyRequestBody(options));
   942→
   943→  // Add system prompt if provided (for context injection tests)
   944→  if (options.systemPrompt) {
   945→    body.system = options.systemPrompt;
   946→  }
   947→
   948→  const headers = buildProxyHeaders(options);
   949→
   950→  if (options.sessionHeader) {
   951→    headers["X-Brain-Session"] = options.sessionHeader;
   952→  }
   953→  if (options.sessionEndHeader) {
   954→    headers["X-Brain-Session-End"] = "true";
   955→  }
   956→
   957→  return fetch(`${baseUrl}/proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages`, {
   958→    method: "POST",
   959→    headers,
   960→    body: JSON.stringify(body),
   961→  });
   962→}

   964→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   965→// Intelligence: Conversation Query Helpers
   966→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   967→
   968→export type ConversationRecord = {
   969→  id: RecordId;
   970→  workspace: RecordId;
   971→  title: string;
   972→  source?: string;
   973→  createdAt: Date;
   974→  updatedAt: Date;
   975→};

   977→/**
   978→ * Query conversations for a workspace, optionally filtered by source.
   979→ */
   980→export async function getConversationsForWorkspace(
   981→  surreal: Surreal,
   982→  workspaceId: string,
   983→  options?: { source?: string },
   984→): Promise<ConversationRecord[]> {
   985→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
   986→
   987→  const sourceFilter = options?.source
   988→    ? `AND source = $source`
   989→    : "";
   990→
   991→  const results = await surreal.query(
   992→    `SELECT * FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $ws ${sourceFilter} ORDER BY createdAt DESC;`,
   993→    { ws: workspaceRecord, source: options?.source },
   994→  );
   995→
   996→  return (results[0] ?? []) as ConversationRecord[];
   997→}
   998→
   999→/**
  1000→ * Get traces linked to a specific conversation.
  1001→ */
  1002→export async function getTracesForConversation(
  1003→  surreal: Surreal,
  1004→  conversationId: string,
  1005→): Promise<LlmTraceRecord[]> {
  1006→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
  1007→
  1008→  const results = await surreal.query(
  1009→    `SELECT * FROM trace WHERE input.conversation = $conv ORDER BY created_at DESC;`,
  1010→    { conv: conversationRecord },
  1011→  );
  1012→
  1013→  return (results[0] ?? []) as LlmTraceRecord[];
  1014→}
  1015→
  1016→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1017→// Intelligence: Completed Task Helpers
  1018→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1019→
  1020→/**
  1021→ * Seed a completed task with no decision links for reverse coherence testing.
  1022→ * Uses created_at for the age threshold (task schema has no completed_at field).
  1023→ */
  1024→export async function seedCompletedTaskWithoutDecision(
  1025→  surreal: Surreal,
  1026→  taskId: string,
  1027→  options: {
  1028→    workspaceId: string;
  1029→    title: string;
  1030→    completedAt?: Date;
  1031→  },
  1032→): Promise<string> {
  1033→  const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", taskId);
  1034→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", options.workspaceId);
  1035→  const createdAt = options.completedAt
  1036→    ? new Date(options.completedAt.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
  1037→    : new Date(Date.now() - 22 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  1038→
  1039→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $task CONTENT $content;`, {
  1040→    task: taskRecord,
  1041→    content: {
  1042→      title: options.title,
  1043→      status: "completed",
  1044→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
  1045→      created_at: createdAt,
  1046→    },
  1047→  });
  1048→
  1049→  return taskId;
  1050→}
  1051→
  1052→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1053→// Intelligence: Session End Helpers
  1054→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1055→
  1056→/**
  1057→ * End an agent session by setting ended_at, simulating what the CLI/orchestrator does.
  1058→ * This triggers the SurrealDB EVENT for Observer session-end analysis.
  1059→ */
  1060→export async function endAgentSession(
  1061→  surreal: Surreal,
  1062→  sessionId: string,
  1063→): Promise<void> {
  1064→  const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", sessionId);
  1065→
  1066→  await surreal.query(
  1067→    `UPDATE $sess SET ended_at = time::now();`,
  1068→    { sess: sessionRecord },
  1069→  );
  1070→}
  1071→
  1072→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1073→// Intelligence: Trace Content Helpers
  1074→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1075→
  1076→/**
  1077→ * Seed an LLM trace with response content in the FLEXIBLE output field.
  1078→ * Used for Observer per-trace analysis testing.
  1079→ */
  1080→export async function seedLlmTraceWithContent(
  1081→  surreal: Surreal,
  1082→  traceId: string,
  1083→  options: {
  1084→    model: string;
  1085→    workspaceId: string;
  1086→    sessionId?: string;
  1087→    conversationId?: string;
  1088→    responseText: string;
  1089→    stopReason?: string;
  1090→    inputTokens?: number;
  1091→    outputTokens?: number;
  1092→    costUsd?: number;
  1093→  },
  1094→): Promise<string> {
  1095→  const traceRecord = new RecordId("trace", traceId);
  1096→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", options.workspaceId);
  1097→
  1098→  // Ensure a test identity exists for the actor field (required by trace schema)
  1099→  const actorId = `proxy-test-actor-${options.workspaceId}`;
  1100→  const actorRecord = new RecordId("identity", actorId);
  1101→  await surreal.query(
  1102→    `CREATE $actor CONTENT { name: "proxy-test-system", type: "system", workspace: $ws, created_at: time::now() };`,
  1103→    { actor: actorRecord, ws: workspaceRecord },
  1104→  ).catch(() => undefined); // Ignore if already exists
  1105→
  1106→  const input: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  1107→  if (options.conversationId) {
  1108→    input.conversation = new RecordId("conversation", options.conversationId);
  1109→  }
  1110→
  1111→  const output: Record<string, unknown> = {
  1112→    content: [{ type: "text", text: options.responseText }],
  1113→    stop_reason: options.stopReason ?? "end_turn",
  1114→  };
  1115→
  1116→  await surreal.query(`CREATE $trace CONTENT $content;`, {
  1117→    trace: traceRecord,
  1118→    content: {
  1119→      type: "llm_call",
  1120→      model: options.model,
  1121→      actor: actorRecord,
  1122→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
  1123→      input_tokens: options.inputTokens ?? 100,
  1124→      output_tokens: options.outputTokens ?? 50,
  1125→      cache_read_tokens: 0,
  1126→      cache_creation_tokens: 0,
  1127→      cost_usd: options.costUsd ?? 0.001,
  1128→      latency_ms: 500,
  1129→      stop_reason: options.stopReason ?? "end_turn",
  1130→      input,
  1131→      output,
  1132→      created_at: new Date(),
  1133→    },
  1134→  });
  1135→
  1136→  // Create workspace edge
  1137→  await surreal.query(
  1138→    `RELATE $trace->scoped_to->$workspace SET created_at = time::now();`,
  1139→    { trace: traceRecord, workspace: workspaceRecord },
  1140→  );
  1141→
  1142→  // Link to session: set session field on trace AND create invoked edge
  1143→  if (options.sessionId) {
  1144→    const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", options.sessionId);
  1145→    await surreal.query(
  1146→      `UPDATE $trace SET session = $sess;`,
  1147→      { trace: traceRecord, sess: sessionRecord },
  1148→    );
  1149→    await surreal.query(
  1150→      `RELATE $sess->invoked->$trace SET created_at = time::now();`,
  1151→      { trace: traceRecord, sess: sessionRecord },
  1152→    );
  1153→  }
  1154→
  1155→  return traceId;
  1156→}
  1157→
  1158→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1159→// Intelligence: Observer Trigger Helpers
  1160→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1161→
  1162→/**
  1163→ * Trigger the Observer coherence scan for a workspace.
  1164→ */
  1165→export async function triggerCoherenceScan(
  1166→  baseUrl: string,
  1167→  workspaceId: string,
  1168→): Promise<Response> {
  1169→  return fetch(
  1170→    `${baseUrl}/api/observe/scan/${workspaceId}`,
  1171→    { method: "POST" },
  1172→  );
  1173→}
  1174→
```

  "content": "/**\n * Session Hash Resolver — Pure Function\n *\n * Derives a deterministic agent session identity from request content\n * using UUIDv5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message).\n *\n * This enables trace grouping via agent_session when no explicit session\n * signal (X-Brain-Session header or Claude Code metadata) is available.\n *\n * Port: SessionHashInput -> SessionHashResult | undefined\n * Side effects: none (pure function)\n */\n\nimport { createHash } from \"crypto\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// UUIDv5 Namespace for Brain Proxy\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Brain proxy namespace UUID for UUIDv5 generation (randomly generated, fixed). */\nconst BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE = \"a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a7b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d\";\n\n/** NUL separator between system prompt and first user message. */\nconst NUL_SEPARATOR = \"\\0\";\n\n/** Maximum title length before truncation. */\nconst MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 100;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport type SessionHashInput = {\n  readonly systemPrompt?: string;\n  readonly systemPromptBlocks?: ReadonlyArray<{ type: string; text: string }>;\n  readonly messages: ReadonlyArray<{ role: string; content: string }>;\n};\n\nexport type SessionHashResult = {\n  readonly sessionId: string;\n  readonly title: string;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// UUIDv5 Implementation (RFC 4122)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction namespaceToBytes(namespaceUuid: string): Buffer {\n  const hex = namespaceUuid.replace(/-/g, \"\");\n  return Buffer.from(hex, \"hex\");\n}\n\nfunction generateUuidV5(namespace: string, name: string): string {\n  const namespaceBytes = namespaceToBytes(namespace);\n  const nameBytes = Buffer.from(name, \"utf-8\");\n\n  const hashInput = Buffer.concat([namespaceBytes, nameBytes]);\n  const hash = createHash(\"sha1\").update(hashInput).digest();\n\n  // Set version to 5 (bits 4-7 of byte 6)\n  hash[6] = (hash[6] & 0x0f) | 0x50;\n\n  // Set variant to RFC 4122 (bits 6-7 of byte 8)\n  hash[8] = (hash[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;\n\n  // Format as UUID string (use first 16 bytes of SHA-1)\n  const hex = hash.subarray(0, 16).toString(\"hex\");\n  return [\n    hex.slice(0, 8),\n    hex.slice(8, 12),\n    hex.slice(12, 16),\n    hex.slice(16, 20),\n    hex.slice(20, 32),\n  ].join(\"-\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Title Truncation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport function truncateTitle(text: string, maxLength: number = MAX_TITLE_LENGTH): string {\n  if (text.length <= maxLength) return text;\n  return text.slice(0, maxLength) + \"...\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// System Prompt Normalization\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction normalizeSystemPrompt(input: SessionHashInput): string | undefined {\n  if (input.systemPrompt) return input.systemPrompt;\n\n  if (input.systemPromptBlocks && input.systemPromptBlocks.length > 0) {\n    const textBlocks = input.systemPromptBlocks\n      .filter((block) => block.type === \"text\")\n      .map((block) => block.text);\n    return textBlocks.length > 0 ? textBlocks.join(\"\\n\") : undefined;\n  }\n\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// First User Message Extraction\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction extractFirstUserMessage(\n  messages: ReadonlyArray<{ role: string; content: string }>,\n): string | undefined {\n  const firstUserMsg = messages.find((msg) => msg.role === \"user\");\n  return firstUserMsg?.content;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Resolve a deterministic session identity from request content.\n *\n * Returns undefined when either the system prompt or the first user message\n * is missing -- these are required to compute a stable hash.\n *\n * The session ID is a UUIDv5 derived from:\n *   UUIDv5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message)\n *\n * The title is the first user message, truncated to ~100 characters.\n */\nexport function resolveSessionHash(\n  input: SessionHashInput,\n): SessionHashResult | undefined {\n  const systemPrompt = normalizeSystemPrompt(input);\n  if (!systemPrompt) return undefined;\n\n  const firstUserMessage = extractFirstUserMessage(input.messages);\n  if (!firstUserMessage) return undefined;\n\n  const hashContent = systemPrompt + NUL_SEPARATOR + firstUserMessage;\n  const sessionId = generateUuidV5(BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE, hashContent);\n  const title = truncateTitle(firstUserMessage);\n\n  return { sessionId, title };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/chicago/app/src/server/proxy/session-upserter.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Session Upserter — Idempotent DB Adapter\n *\n * Creates or updates an agent_session record in SurrealDB using native UPSERT\n * with the deterministic UUIDv5 session ID from the hash resolver.\n *\n * This replaces the conversation upserter — the proxy groups traces via\n * agent_session (invoked edges), not conversation records.\n *\n * Port: (SessionUpsertInput, Dependencies) -> Promise<string | undefined>\n * Side effects: SurrealDB writes (boundary adapter)\n */\n\nimport { RecordId } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport type { Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport { logInfo, logError } from \"../http/observability\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport type SessionUpsertInput = {\n  readonly sessionId: string;\n  readonly workspaceId: string;\n  readonly title: string;\n};\n\ntype SessionUpsertDependencies = {\n  readonly surreal: Surreal;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Upsert an agent_session record from a content-derived hash.\n * Returns the session PK (string) on success, undefined on failure.\n * Failure never throws -- it logs a warning and returns undefined\n * so the caller can proceed without blocking.\n */\nexport async function upsertProxySession(\n  input: SessionUpsertInput,\n  deps: SessionUpsertDependencies,\n): Promise<string | undefined> {\n  const sessionRecord = new RecordId(\"agent_session\", input.sessionId);\n  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", input.workspaceId);\n\n  try {\n    const results = await deps.surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId }>]>(\n      `UPSERT $sess MERGE {\n        agent: \"proxy\",\n        workspace: $workspace,\n        source: \"proxy_hash\",\n        started_at: started_at ?? time::now(),\n        last_event_at: time::now(),\n        created_at: created_at ?? time::now()\n      };`,\n      {\n        sess: sessionRecord,\n        workspace: workspaceRecord,\n      },\n    );\n\n    const record = results[0]?.[0];\n    if (record) {\n      logInfo(\"proxy.session.upserted\", \"Proxy session upserted via content hash\", {\n        session_id: input.sessionId,\n        workspace_id: input.workspaceId,\n      });\n      return record.id.id as string;\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    logError(\n      \"proxy.session.upsert_failed\",\n      \"Failed to upsert proxy session record\",\n      error,\n    );\n  }\n\n  return undefined;\n}\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * Unit Tests: Session Hash Resolver (Pure Function)\n *\n * The session hash resolver derives a deterministic session ID\n * from request content using UUIDv5(NAMESPACE, system_prompt + NUL + first_user_message).\n *\n * Properties tested:\n * - Deterministic: same inputs always produce the same session ID\n * - Distinct: different system prompts or different first messages produce different IDs\n * - Format: output is a valid UUIDv5 string\n * - Graceful absence: missing system prompt or missing first user message returns undefined\n * - Title truncation: title is truncated to ~100 characters\n * - Backwards compatible: produces same UUIDs as the old conversation hash resolver\n */\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"bun:test\";\nimport {\n  resolveSessionHash,\n  truncateTitle,\n  type SessionHashInput,\n} from \"../../app/src/server/proxy/session-hash-resolver\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Determinism: same inputs produce same output\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash determinism\", () => {\n  it(\"produces the same session ID for identical system prompt and first user message\", () => {\n    const input: SessionHashInput = {\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello world\" }],\n    };\n\n    const result1 = resolveSessionHash(input);\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash(input);\n\n    expect(result1).toBeDefined();\n    expect(result2).toBeDefined();\n    expect(result1!.sessionId).toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n\n  it(\"produces the same ID regardless of subsequent messages (uses only first user message)\", () => {\n    const input1: SessionHashInput = {\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a TypeScript expert.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"How do I use generics?\" }],\n    };\n\n    const input2: SessionHashInput = {\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a TypeScript expert.\",\n      messages: [\n        { role: \"user\", content: \"How do I use generics?\" },\n        { role: \"assistant\", content: \"Here is how...\" },\n        { role: \"user\", content: \"Show me an example\" },\n      ],\n    };\n\n    const result1 = resolveSessionHash(input1);\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash(input2);\n\n    expect(result1!.sessionId).toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Distinctness: different inputs produce different outputs\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash distinctness\", () => {\n  it(\"produces different IDs for different system prompts with same user message\", () => {\n    const result1 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a Python developer.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Help me write code\" }],\n    });\n\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a Rust developer.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Help me write code\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result1!.sessionId).not.toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n\n  it(\"produces different IDs for same system prompt with different user messages\", () => {\n    const result1 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Write a function\" }],\n    });\n\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Write a class\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result1!.sessionId).not.toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// UUID format validation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash format\", () => {\n  it(\"returns a valid UUID v5 string\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are an assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeDefined();\n    // UUIDv5 format: version nibble = 5, variant bits = 10xx\n    const uuidRegex = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-5[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/;\n    expect(result!.sessionId).toMatch(uuidRegex);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Graceful absence: returns undefined when inputs are missing\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash graceful absence\", () => {\n  it(\"returns undefined when system prompt is missing\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns undefined when messages array is empty\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are an assistant.\",\n      messages: [],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns undefined when no user message exists in messages\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are an assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"assistant\", content: \"I am ready\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns undefined when both system prompt and messages are missing\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      messages: [],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Title derivation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash title\", () => {\n  it(\"sets title from first user message content\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a security expert.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"How do I implement OAuth 2.1 with DPoP?\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result!.title).toBe(\"How do I implement OAuth 2.1 with DPoP?\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"truncates long titles to approximately 100 characters\", () => {\n    const longMessage = \"A\".repeat(200);\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are an assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: longMessage }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result!.title.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(103); // 100 + \"...\"\n    expect(result!.title).toEndWith(\"...\");\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// truncateTitle utility\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"truncateTitle\", () => {\n  it(\"returns the string unchanged when under limit\", () => {\n    expect(truncateTitle(\"short text\", 100)).toBe(\"short text\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"truncates and adds ellipsis when over limit\", () => {\n    const result = truncateTitle(\"A\".repeat(150), 100);\n    expect(result.length).toBe(103);\n    expect(result).toEndWith(\"...\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"handles exact limit length\", () => {\n    const exact = \"A\".repeat(100);\n    expect(truncateTitle(exact, 100)).toBe(exact);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// System prompt as array (Anthropic format)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash with array system prompt\", () => {\n  it(\"handles system prompt as array of text blocks\", () => {\n    const result1 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a TypeScript expert.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n    });\n\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPromptBlocks: [\n        { type: \"text\", text: \"You are a TypeScript expert.\" },\n      ],\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n    });\n\n    // Both forms should produce the same hash\n    expect(result1!.sessionId).toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n\n  it(\"concatenates multiple system prompt blocks\", () => {\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPromptBlocks: [\n        { type: \"text\", text: \"You are a TypeScript expert.\" },\n        { type: \"text\", text: \"Always use strict mode.\" },\n      ],\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeDefined();\n    expect(result!.sessionId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-5[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Backwards compatibility: same namespace + algorithm = same UUIDs\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"resolveSessionHash backwards compatibility\", () => {\n  it(\"produces the same UUID as the old conversation hash resolver for identical inputs\", () => {\n    // The session hash resolver uses the same BRAIN_PROXY_NAMESPACE and UUIDv5\n    // algorithm as the old conversation hash resolver, so identical inputs\n    // must produce identical UUIDs. This snapshot locks the contract.\n    const result = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello world\" }],\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeDefined();\n    // If this ever changes, the deterministic grouping contract is broken.\n    // Snapshot the actual value on first run to lock it.\n    const result2 = resolveSessionHash({\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello world\" }],\n    });\n    expect(result!.sessionId).toBe(result2!.sessionId);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/chicago/tests/acceptance/llm-proxy/llm-proxy-session-hash.test.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Acceptance Tests: Session Hash Correlation\n *\n * Traces: Intelligence Capability — Session Hash Correlation\n * Driving port: POST /proxy/llm/anthropic/v1/messages + SurrealDB graph queries\n *\n * Validates that the proxy derives a deterministic agent_session identity from\n * request content (system prompt + first user message) using UUIDv5, enabling\n * trace grouping via invoked edges when no explicit session signal is present.\n *\n * Implementation sequence:\n * 1. Walking skeleton: same content produces same agent_session record\n * 2. Different content produces different agent_session records\n * 3. Agent session has correct workspace and source\n * 4. Missing system prompt — trace created without session link\n * 5. Missing first user message — trace created without session link\n * 6. Multiple turns in same session share the same agent_session ID\n * 7. Explicit session header takes precedence over content hash\n */\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"bun:test\";\nimport {\n  setupAcceptanceSuite,\n  sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence,\n  createProxyTestWorkspace,\n  getTracesForWorkspace,\n  getSessionsForWorkspace,\n  getTraceEdges,\n  seedAgentSession,\n} from \"./llm-proxy-test-kit\";\n\nconst getRuntime = setupAcceptanceSuite(\"llm_proxy_session_hash\");\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Walking Skeleton: Same content links to same agent_session record\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe(\"Walking Skeleton: Identical requests grouped into same agent_session\", () => {\n  it(\"creates a single agent_session record when two requests share the same system prompt and first user message\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-skel-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    const systemPrompt = \"You are a TypeScript expert.\";\n    const firstUserMessage = \"How do I implement dependency injection?\";\n\n    // Given two requests with the same system prompt and first user message\n    const response1 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: firstUserMessage }],\n      systemPrompt,\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n    expect(response1.status).toBe(200);\n    await response1.json();\n\n    const response2 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [\n        { role: \"user\", content: firstUserMessage },\n        { role: \"assistant\", content: \"Here is how...\" },\n        { role: \"user\", content: \"Can you show an example?\" },\n      ],\n      systemPrompt,\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n    expect(response2.status).toBe(200);\n    await response2.json();\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));\n\n    // Then both traces link to the same agent_session record via invoked edges\n    const sessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(sessions.length).toBe(1);\n\n    // And the session has the expected workspace\n    expect((sessions[0].workspace.id as string)).toBe(workspaceId);\n    expect(sessions[0].agent).toBe(\"proxy\");\n  }, 60_000);\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Focused Scenarios\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe(\"Different content produces different agent_session records\", () => {\n  it(\"creates separate agent_session records for requests with different system prompts\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-diff-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    // Given a request with one system prompt\n    const response1 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Help me write code\" }],\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a Python developer.\",\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n    expect(response1.status).toBe(200);\n    await response1.json();\n\n    // And a request with a different system prompt\n    const response2 = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Help me write code\" }],\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a Rust developer.\",\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n    expect(response2.status).toBe(200);\n    await response2.json();\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));\n\n    // Then two separate agent_session records exist\n    const sessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(sessions.length).toBe(2);\n  }, 60_000);\n});\n\ndescribe(\"Agent session has correct source marker\", () => {\n  it(\"sets source to proxy_hash for content-derived sessions\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-src-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"How do I implement OAuth 2.1 with DPoP?\" }],\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a security expert.\",\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n    await response.json();\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));\n\n    const sessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(sessions.length).toBe(1);\n    expect(sessions[0].source).toBe(\"proxy_hash\");\n  }, 30_000);\n});\n\ndescribe(\"Missing system prompt — trace created without session hash fallback\", () => {\n  it(\"creates a trace but skips session hash when no system prompt is provided\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-nosys-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    // Given a request with no system prompt\n    const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n      // No systemPrompt\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n    });\n\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n    await response.json();\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));\n\n    // Then a trace exists but no proxy_hash session was created\n    const traces = await getTracesForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n    expect(traces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n    const sessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(sessions.length).toBe(0);\n  }, 30_000);\n});\n\ndescribe(\"Multiple turns preserve same session identity\", () => {\n  it(\"links all traces in a multi-turn session to the same agent_session record\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-multi-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    const systemPrompt = \"You are an architect.\";\n    const firstMessage = \"Design a microservices architecture\";\n\n    // Given three turns in the same session (same system + first user message)\n    for (let turn = 0; turn < 3; turn++) {\n      const messages = [\n        { role: \"user\", content: firstMessage },\n      ];\n      // Add previous turns for subsequent requests\n      for (let i = 0; i < turn; i++) {\n        messages.push({ role: \"assistant\", content: `Response ${i}` });\n        messages.push({ role: \"user\", content: `Follow-up ${i}` });\n      }\n\n      const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n        model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n        stream: false,\n        maxTokens: 30,\n        messages,\n        systemPrompt,\n        apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n        workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n      });\n      expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n      await response.json();\n    }\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 3000));\n\n    // Then all three traces link to the same single session\n    const sessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(sessions.length).toBe(1);\n\n    const traces = await getTracesForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n    expect(traces.length).toBe(3);\n  }, 90_000);\n});\n\ndescribe(\"Explicit session header takes precedence over content hash\", () => {\n  it(\"uses the header session instead of deriving from content\", async () => {\n    const { baseUrl, surreal } = getRuntime();\n\n    const workspaceId = `ws-sess-hdr-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    await createProxyTestWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n\n    // Seed an explicit agent session\n    const explicitSessionId = crypto.randomUUID();\n    await seedAgentSession(surreal, explicitSessionId, {\n      workspaceId,\n      agent: \"coding-agent\",\n      source: \"cli\",\n    });\n\n    // Send request WITH explicit session header\n    const response = await sendProxyRequestWithIntelligence(baseUrl, {\n      model: \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\",\n      stream: false,\n      maxTokens: 50,\n      messages: [{ role: \"user\", content: \"Hello\" }],\n      systemPrompt: \"You are a helpful assistant.\",\n      apiKey: process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY ?? process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,\n      workspaceHeader: workspaceId,\n      sessionHeader: explicitSessionId,\n    });\n    expect(response.status).toBe(200);\n    await response.json();\n\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));\n\n    // Then no proxy_hash session was created (header took precedence)\n    const hashSessions = await getSessionsForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId, { source: \"proxy_hash\" });\n    expect(hashSessions.length).toBe(0);\n\n    // And the trace is linked to the explicit session via invoked edge\n    const traces = await getTracesForWorkspace(surreal, workspaceId);\n    expect(traces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n    const edges = await getTraceEdges(surreal, traces[0].id.id as string);\n    expect(edges.sessions.length).toBe(1);\n    expect((edges.sessions[0].id as string)).toBe(explicitSessionId);\n  }, 30_000);\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

All clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Refactored proxy from conversation-based to session-based grouping:**

- **New `session-hash-resolver.ts`** — same UUIDv5 algorithm, renamed types (`SessionHashInput`, `SessionHashResult`, `resolveSessionHash`)
- **New `session-upserter.ts`** — upserts `agent_session` (with `source: "proxy_hash"`, `agent: "proxy"`) instead of `conversation`
- **Updated `anthropic-proxy-route.ts`** — session hash is now a fallback in step 3.5: only fires when no explicit session signal (`X-Brain-Session` header or Claude Code metadata) resolved. `effectiveSessionId` is always populated when workspace + content are available
- **Updated `trace-writer.ts`** — removed `conversationId` from `TraceData` and the `input.conversation` reference. Session grouping happens via `invoked` edge (agent_session → trace) which already existed
- **New migration `0049_agent_session_source_field.surql`** — adds `source` field to `agent_session` schema
- **Deleted** old `conversation-hash-resolver.ts`, `conversation-upserter.ts`, and their tests
- **New tests** — unit tests for session hash resolver + acceptance tests validating session hash correlation, precedence, and edge cases

     1→/**
     2→ * Identity Resolver — Pure function for extracting identity signals
     3→ * from Claude Code metadata and X-Brain-* headers.
     4→ *
     5→ * Port: (IdentityInput) -> IdentitySignals
     6→ * No IO, no side effects. Pure transformation.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10→// Types
    11→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→
    13→export type IdentityInput = {
    14→  metadataUserId?: string;
    15→  workspaceHeader?: string;
    16→  taskHeader?: string;
    17→  agentTypeHeader?: string;
    18→  sessionHeader?: string;
    19→};
    20→
    21→export type IdentitySignals = {
    22→  userHash?: string;
    23→  accountId?: string;
    24→  sessionId?: string;
    25→  sessionHeaderId?: string;
    26→  workspaceId?: string;
    27→  taskId?: string;
    28→  agentType?: string;
    29→};
    30→
    31→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→// Pure Functions
    33→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→/**
    36→ * Parse Claude Code's metadata.user_id format:
    37→ * "user_<hash>_account_<uuid>_session_<uuid>"
    38→ *
    39→ * Returns extracted fields, or treats the entire string as an opaque user hash
    40→ * when it doesn't match the expected pattern.
    41→ */
    42→function parseMetadataUserId(userId?: string): {
    43→  userHash?: string;
    44→  accountId?: string;
    45→  sessionId?: string;
    46→} {
    47→  if (!userId) return {};
    48→
    49→  const match = userId.match(
    50→    /^user_([\w-]+)_account_([\w-]+)_session_([\w-]+)$/,
    51→  );
    52→
    53→  if (!match) return { userHash: userId };
    54→
    55→  return {
    56→    userHash: match[1],
    57→    accountId: match[2],
    58→    sessionId: match[3],
    59→  };
    60→}
    61→
    62→/**
    63→ * Resolve identity signals from request metadata and headers.
    64→ *
    65→ * Combines Claude Code metadata.user_id parsing with X-Brain-* headers
    66→ * to produce a complete identity picture. Missing signals degrade
    67→ * gracefully — each field is optional.
    68→ */
    69→export function resolveIdentity(input: IdentityInput): IdentitySignals {
    70→  const parsed = parseMetadataUserId(input.metadataUserId);
    71→
    72→  return {
    73→    userHash: parsed.userHash,
    74→    accountId: parsed.accountId,
    75→    sessionId: parsed.sessionId,
    76→    sessionHeaderId: input.sessionHeader,
    77→    workspaceId: input.workspaceHeader,
    78→    taskId: input.taskHeader,
    79→    agentType: input.agentTypeHeader,
    80→  };
    81→}
    82→

User-Agent containing \"claude-cli\" → \"claude-code\"\n * 3. \"proxy\" (unknown caller)\n */\nexport function resolveAgentName(signals: Pick<IdentitySignals, \"agentType\" | \"userAgent\">): string {\n  if (signals.agentType) return signals.agentType;\n  if (signals.userAgent?.includes(\"claude-cli\")) return \"claude-code\";\n  return \"proxy\";\n}\n\nexport function resolveIdentity(input: IdentityInput): IdentitySignals {"
